==> src/features/admin/UsuariosAdministracion.tsx <==
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { AlertCircle, ArrowDownWideNarrow, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, RefreshCw, Search, ShieldCheck, UserCog, UserRoundPlus, UserX } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { useAuth } from "@/features/auth/AuthProvider";
import { roleLabels, type UserRole } from "@/features/auth/roles";

type UsuarioListado = {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  nombre: string;
  rol: UserRole;
  is_active: boolean;
  telefono: string | null;
  descripcion: string | null;
  created_at: string;
};

const roleOptions: UserRole[] = ["ADMIN", "SUPERVISOR", "OPERADOR"];
const pageSize = 10;

export function UsuariosAdministracion() {
  const { client, profile } = useAuth();
  const [usuarios, setUsuarios] = useState<UsuarioListado[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [savingId, setSavingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [roleFilter, setRoleFilter] = useState<UserRole | "all">("all");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<"all" | "active" | "inactive">("all");
  const [dateOrder, setDateOrder] = useState<"newest" | "oldest">("newest");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);

  const isAdmin = profile?.rol === "ADMIN";

  const loadUsuarios = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!client) {
      setError("No está disponible la conexión con Supabase.");
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const { data, error: requestError } = await client
        .from("usuarios")
        .select("id, email, nombre, rol, is_active, telefono, descripcion, created_at")
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

      if (requestError) throw new Error(requestError.message);
      setUsuarios((data ?? []) as UsuarioListado[]);
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "No se pudo cargar la lista de usuarios.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [client]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadUsuarios();
  }, [loadUsuarios]);

  const canEditUser = (usuario: UsuarioListado) => usuario.id !== profile?.id;

  async function updateRole(usuario: UsuarioListado, nextRole: UserRole) {
    if (!client || !isAdmin) return;
    if (!canEditUser(usuario) && nextRole !== "ADMIN") {
      setError("No puedes quitarte el rol de administrador a tu propia cuenta.");
      return;
    }

    setSavingId(usuario.id);
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    try {
      const { error: requestError } = await client.rpc("admin_set_usuario_rol", {
        p_user_id: usuario.id,
        p_rol: nextRole,
      });

      if (requestError) throw new Error(requestError.message);
      setUsuarios((items) => items.map((item) => (item.id === usuario.id ? { ...item, rol: nextRole } : item)));
      setNotice(`Rol actualizado para ${usuario.nombre} a ${roleLabels[nextRole]}.`);
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "No se pudo actualizar el rol.");
    } finally {
      setSavingId(null);
    }
  }

  async function toggleUserState(usuario: UsuarioListado) {
    if (!client || !isAdmin) return;
    if (!canEditUser(usuario)) {
      setError("No puedes desactivar tu propia cuenta desde la administración.");
      return;
    }

    setSavingId(usuario.id);
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    try {
      const { error: requestError } = await client.rpc("admin_set_usuario_estado", {
        p_user_id: usuario.id,
        p_is_active: !usuario.is_active,
      });

      if (requestError) throw new Error(requestError.message);
      setUsuarios((items) => items.map((item) => (item.id === usuario.id ? { ...item, is_active: !item.is_active } : item)));
      setNotice(`${usuario.nombre} quedó ${!usuario.is_active ? "activo" : "inactivo"}.`);
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "No se pudo cambiar el estado del usuario.");
    } finally {
      setSavingId(null);
    }
  }

  const summary = useMemo(() => {
    const total = usuarios.length;
    const activos = usuarios.filter((usuario) => usuario.is_active).length;
    const corteReciente = Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    const recientes = usuarios.filter((usuario) => {
      const createdAt = Date.parse(usuario.created_at);
      return Number.isFinite(createdAt) && createdAt >= corteReciente;
    }).length;
    const porRol = roleOptions.map((rol) => ({
      rol,
      total: usuarios.filter((usuario) => usuario.rol === rol).length,
    }));
    return { total, activos, inactivos: total - activos, recientes, porRol };
  }, [usuarios]);

  const filteredUsuarios = useMemo(() => {
    const normalizedQuery = query.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("es");
    return usuarios.filter((usuario) => {
      const matchesQuery = !normalizedQuery || [usuario.nombre, usuario.email, usuario.telefono, usuario.descripcion]
        .some((value) => value?.toLocaleLowerCase("es").includes(normalizedQuery));
      const matchesRole = roleFilter === "all" || usuario.rol === roleFilter;
      const matchesStatus = statusFilter === "all"
        || (statusFilter === "active" ? usuario.is_active : !usuario.is_active);
      return matchesQuery && matchesRole && matchesStatus;
    });
  }, [usuarios, query, roleFilter, statusFilter]);

  const sortedUsuarios = useMemo(() => [...filteredUsuarios].sort((left, right) => {
    const difference = Date.parse(left.created_at) - Date.parse(right.created_at);
    return dateOrder === "newest" ? -difference : difference;
  }), [filteredUsuarios, dateOrder]);

  useEffect(() => { setPage(1); }, [query, roleFilter, statusFilter, dateOrder]);

  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filteredUsuarios.length / pageSize));
  const currentPage = Math.min(page, pageCount);
  const visibleUsuarios = sortedUsuarios.slice((currentPage - 1) * pageSize, currentPage * pageSize);

  if (!isAdmin) {
    return <Card className="rounded-2xl border-red-200 bg-red-50 shadow-none"><CardContent className="flex items-start gap-3 p-5 text-red-950"><AlertCircle size={20} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" /><div><p className="font-bold">Acceso restringido</p><p className="mt-1 text-sm">Esta vista solo está disponible para administradores activos.</p></div></CardContent></Card>;
  }

  return (
    <section className="space-y-5" aria-labelledby="usuarios-admin-title">
      <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.18em] text-[#2D6A4F]">Administración</p>
          <h1 id="usuarios-admin-title" className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-[#17251D]">Usuarios del sistema</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#526157]">Control de acceso, estado activo y distribución de roles.</p>
        </div>
        <Button type="button" variant="outline" className="min-h-11 self-start rounded-xl sm:self-auto" onClick={() => void loadUsuarios()} disabled={loading}>
          <RefreshCw size={16} className={loading ? "animate-spin" : ""} /> Actualizar
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <StatCard label="Usuarios registrados" value={summary.total.toLocaleString("es-PE")} detail="Cuentas en el sistema" accent="bg-[#E8EEE7] text-[#1B4332]" />
        <StatCard label="Cuentas activas" value={summary.activos.toLocaleString("es-PE")} detail={`${summary.total ? Math.round((summary.activos / summary.total) * 100) : 0}% del total`} accent="bg-[#DCF4E4] text-[#1B4332]" />
        <StatCard label="Cuentas inactivas" value={summary.inactivos.toLocaleString("es-PE")} detail="Sin acceso habilitado" accent="bg-[#F5E7D1] text-[#7A4A14]" />
        <StatCard label="Altas últimos 30 días" value={summary.recientes.toLocaleString("es-PE")} detail="Según fecha de registro" accent="bg-[#E8EEF3] text-[#31556F]" />
      </div>

      <Card className="rounded-2xl">
        <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2"><UserRoundPlus size={18} /> Distribución por rol</CardTitle><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#526157]">Cuentas registradas por nivel de acceso.</p></CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
          {summary.porRol.map(({ rol, total }) => {
            const percentage = summary.total ? (total / summary.total) * 100 : 0;
            return <div key={rol}>
              <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm"><span className="font-semibold text-[#17251D]">{roleLabels[rol]}</span><span className="tabular-nums text-[#526157]">{total} · {Math.round(percentage)}%</span></div>
              <div role="img" aria-label={`${roleLabels[rol]}: ${total} de ${summary.total} usuarios`} className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[#E8EEE7]"><div className="h-full rounded-full bg-[#2D6A4F]" style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }} /></div>
            </div>;
          })}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {error && <Card className="rounded-2xl border-red-200 bg-red-50 shadow-none"><CardContent className="p-4 text-sm text-red-950">{error}</CardContent></Card>}
      {notice && <Card className="rounded-2xl border-green-200 bg-green-50 shadow-none"><CardContent className="p-4 text-sm text-green-950">{notice}</CardContent></Card>}

      <Card className="rounded-2xl">
        <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2"><UserCog size={18} /> Listado de usuarios</CardTitle><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#526157]">Busca por nombre, correo, teléfono o descripción, y filtra por rol o estado.</p></CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="p-0">
          {loading ? (
            <p className="px-5 pb-5 text-sm text-[#526157]">Cargando usuarios…</p>
          ) : usuarios.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="px-5 pb-5 text-sm text-[#526157]">No hay usuarios registrados aún.</p>
          ) : (
            <>
            <div className="grid gap-3 border-y border-[#17251D]/10 bg-[#F5F8F4]/60 p-4 sm:grid-cols-[minmax(220px,1fr)_180px_170px_190px]">
              <label className="relative block">
                <span className="sr-only">Buscar usuarios</span>
                <Search size={16} aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#526157]" />
                <input type="search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Buscar usuarios…" className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17251D]/15 bg-white pl-10 pr-3 text-sm text-[#17251D] outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20" />
              </label>
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-[#526157]">
                <span className="sr-only">Filtrar por rol</span>
                <select value={roleFilter} onChange={(event) => setRoleFilter(event.target.value as UserRole | "all")} className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17251D]/15 bg-white px-3 text-sm font-medium text-[#17251D] outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20">
                  <option value="all">Todos los roles</option>
                  {roleOptions.map((rol) => <option key={rol} value={rol}>{roleLabels[rol]}</option>)}
                </select>
              </label>
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-[#526157]">
                <span className="sr-only">Filtrar por estado</span>
                <select value={statusFilter} onChange={(event) => setStatusFilter(event.target.value as "all" | "active" | "inactive")} className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17251D]/15 bg-white px-3 text-sm font-medium text-[#17251D] outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20">
                  <option value="all">Todos los estados</option><option value="active">Activos</option><option value="inactive">Inactivos</option>
                </select>
              </label>
              <label className="relative block">
                <span className="sr-only">Ordenar usuarios por fecha de registro</span>
                <ArrowDownWideNarrow size={16} aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#526157]" />
                <select value={dateOrder} onChange={(event) => setDateOrder(event.target.value as "newest" | "oldest")} className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17251D]/15 bg-white pl-10 pr-3 text-sm font-medium text-[#17251D] outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20">
                  <option value="newest">Registro más reciente</option><option value="oldest">Registro más antiguo</option>
                </select>
              </label>
            </div>
            {filteredUsuarios.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="px-5 py-8 text-center text-sm text-[#526157]">No hay usuarios que coincidan con la búsqueda y los filtros.</p>
            ) : <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[780px] text-left text-sm">
                <thead className="border-b border-[#17251D]/10 bg-[#F5F8F4] text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-[#526157]">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="px-5 py-3">Usuario</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-3">Email</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-3">Rol</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-3">Estado</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-3">Registro</th>
                    <th className="px-5 py-3 text-right">Acción</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#17251D]/10">
                  {visibleUsuarios.map((usuario) => (
                    <tr key={usuario.id} className="align-top hover:bg-[#F5F8F4]/70">
                      <td className="px-5 py-4">
                        <div className="font-semibold text-[#17251D]">{usuario.nombre}</div>
                        {usuario.telefono && <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#526157]">{usuario.telefono}</div>}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-4 text-[#17251D]">
                        <span className="block">{usuario.email}</span>
                        {usuario.descripcion && <span className="mt-1 block max-w-xs text-xs text-[#526157]">{usuario.descripcion}</span>}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-4">
                        <label className="sr-only" htmlFor={`rol-${usuario.id}`}>Cambiar rol de {usuario.nombre}</label>
                        <select
                          id={`rol-${usuario.id}`}
                          value={usuario.rol}
                          disabled={savingId === usuario.id || !canEditUser(usuario)}
                          onChange={(event) => void updateRole(usuario, event.target.value as UserRole)}
                          className="min-h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17251D]/15 bg-white px-3 text-sm text-[#17251D] outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-[#F6F8F7]"
                        >
                          {roleOptions.map((rol) => (
                            <option key={rol} value={rol}>{roleLabels[rol]}</option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-4">
                        <span className={`inline-flex rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${usuario.is_active ? "bg-[#DCF4E4] text-[#1B4332]" : "bg-[#F5E7D1] text-[#7A4A14]"}`}>
                          {usuario.is_active ? "Activo" : "Inactivo"}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-4 text-xs text-[#526157]">{Number.isFinite(Date.parse(usuario.created_at)) ? new Date(usuario.created_at).toLocaleDateString("es-PE") : "Sin fecha"}</td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-4 text-right">
                        <Button
                          type="button"
                          variant={usuario.is_active ? "outline" : "primary"}
                          className="min-h-10 rounded-xl"
                          disabled={savingId === usuario.id || !canEditUser(usuario)}
                          onClick={() => void toggleUserState(usuario)}
                        >
                          {usuario.is_active ? <UserX size={15} /> : <ShieldCheck size={15} />}
                          <span className="ml-2">{usuario.is_active ? "Desactivar" : "Activar"}</span>
                        </Button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>}
            {filteredUsuarios.length > 0 && <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-[#17251D]/10 px-4 py-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <p className="text-xs text-[#526157]">Mostrando {(currentPage - 1) * pageSize + 1}–{Math.min(currentPage * pageSize, filteredUsuarios.length)} de {filteredUsuarios.length} usuarios</p>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Button type="button" variant="outline" className="min-h-9 rounded-lg px-3" aria-label="Página anterior" onClick={() => setPage((value) => Math.max(1, value - 1))} disabled={currentPage === 1}><ChevronLeft size={16} /> Anterior</Button>
                <span className="min-w-16 text-center text-xs tabular-nums text-[#526157]">{currentPage} / {pageCount}</span>
                <Button type="button" variant="outline" className="min-h-9 rounded-lg px-3" aria-label="Página siguiente" onClick={() => setPage((value) => Math.min(pageCount, value + 1))} disabled={currentPage >= pageCount}>Siguiente <ChevronRight size={16} /></Button>
              </div>
            </div>}
            </>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </section>
  );
}

function StatCard({ label, value, detail, accent }: { label: string; value: string; detail: string; accent: string }) {
  return (
    <Card className="rounded-2xl">
      <CardContent className="p-5">
        <div className={`inline-flex rounded-full px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[.12em] ${accent}`}>{label}</div>
        <p className="mt-4 text-3xl font-bold tabular-nums text-[#17251D]">{value}</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[#526157]">{detail}</p>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

==> src/features/assistant/ContextualAssistant.tsx <==
"use client";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import type { Lote } from "@/components/lotes/LotesDataTable";
import { GeminiChat } from "@/features/assistant/GeminiChat";

export function ContextualAssistant({ lote, open, onClose }: { lote: Lote | null; open: boolean; onClose: () => void }) {
  const panel = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const close = useRef(onClose);
  close.current = onClose;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open || !lote) return;
    const opener = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? document.activeElement : null;
    panel.current?.focus();
    function keydown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (event.key === "Escape") { event.preventDefault(); close.current(); }
      if (event.key !== "Tab" || !panel.current) return;
      const items = panel.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('button:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled])');
      const first = items[0]; const last = items[items.length - 1];
      if (!first) return;
      if (event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === first || document.activeElement === panel.current)) { event.preventDefault(); last.focus(); }
      else if (!event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === last || document.activeElement === panel.current)) { event.preventDefault(); first.focus(); }
    }
    document.addEventListener("keydown", keydown);
    return () => { document.removeEventListener("keydown", keydown); opener?.focus(); };
  }, [open, lote]);
  if (!open || !lote) return null;
  return <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex justify-end bg-[#17251D]/50" onMouseDown={(event) => { if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose(); }}>
    <aside ref={panel} role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Asistente del lote" tabIndex={-1} className="h-[100dvh] w-full max-w-md overflow-y-auto bg-[#F5F8F4] p-4 outline-none">
      <button onClick={onClose} className="mb-3 rounded-lg border border-[#526157]/30 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#2D6A4F]">Cerrar asistente</button>
      <GeminiChat lotId={lote.id} title="Asistente del lote" />
    </aside>
  </div>;
}

==> src/features/assistant/GeminiChat.tsx <==
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useId, useRef, useState, type FormEvent, type KeyboardEvent } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import { Bot, ChevronDown, LoaderCircle, MessageCircle, Minimize2, Send, X } from "lucide-react";
import { AssistantRequestError, askGemini, savedLotId, type AssistantFailureKind } from "./assistant-api";
import { useAuth } from "@/features/auth/AuthProvider";

type Message = { role: "user" | "assistant"; text: string };
type Presentation = "inline" | "floating";

export function GeminiChat({
  lotId,
  title = "Asistente agrícola",
  embedded = false,
  presentation = "inline",
  open,
  onOpenChange,
}: {
  lotId?: string | null;
  title?: string;
  embedded?: boolean;
  presentation?: Presentation;
  open?: boolean;
  onOpenChange?: (open: boolean) => void;
}) {
  const { session } = useAuth();
  const id = savedLotId(lotId);
  const conversationKey = `${session?.user.id ?? "guest"}:${id ?? "general"}`;
  if (presentation === "floating") {
    return <FloatingAssistant key={conversationKey} lotId={id} title={title} open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} />;
  }
  return <Conversation key={conversationKey} lotId={id} title={title} embedded={embedded} presentation="inline" />;
}

function FloatingAssistant({
  lotId,
  title,
  open,
  onOpenChange,
}: {
  lotId: string | null;
  title: string;
  open?: boolean;
  onOpenChange?: (open: boolean) => void;
}) {
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);
  const [internalOpen, setInternalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [mobile, setMobile] = useState(false);
  const [visualViewport, setVisualViewport] = useState<{ top: number; height: number } | null>(null);
  const panel = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const opener = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
  const isOpen = open ?? internalOpen;
  const changeOpen = useCallback((next: boolean) => {
    if (next && !isOpen) opener.current = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? document.activeElement : null;
    setInternalOpen(next);
    onOpenChange?.(next);
  }, [isOpen, onOpenChange]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
    const media = window.matchMedia("(max-width: 639px)");
    const update = () => {
      setMobile(media.matches);
      const viewport = window.visualViewport;
      setVisualViewport(viewport
        ? { top: viewport.offsetTop, height: viewport.height }
        : { top: 0, height: window.innerHeight });
    };
    update();
    media.addEventListener("change", update);
    window.visualViewport?.addEventListener("resize", update);
    window.visualViewport?.addEventListener("scroll", update);
    window.addEventListener("resize", update);
    return () => {
      media.removeEventListener("change", update);
      window.visualViewport?.removeEventListener("resize", update);
      window.visualViewport?.removeEventListener("scroll", update);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", update);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    if (!opener.current) opener.current = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? document.activeElement : null;
    const frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => panel.current?.focus());
    function onKeyDown(event: globalThis.KeyboardEvent) {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        event.preventDefault();
        changeOpen(false);
        return;
      }
      if (event.key !== "Tab" || !mobile || !panel.current) return;
      const items = panel.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('button:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), a[href]');
      const first = items[0];
      const last = items[items.length - 1];
      if (event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === first || document.activeElement === panel.current)) {
        event.preventDefault();
        last?.focus();
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === last || document.activeElement === panel.current)) {
        event.preventDefault();
        first?.focus();
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => {
      window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
      opener.current?.focus();
      opener.current = null;
    };
  }, [isOpen, mobile, changeOpen]);

  if (!mounted) return null;
  return createPortal(<div className={`pointer-events-none fixed inset-0 z-[70] ${isOpen ? "isolate" : ""}`}>
    {isOpen && mobile && <button type="button" aria-label="Cerrar asistente" tabIndex={-1} onClick={() => changeOpen(false)} className="pointer-events-auto fixed inset-0 bg-[#17251D]/55" />}
    <div
      ref={panel}
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal={mobile ? true : undefined}
      aria-label={title}
      tabIndex={-1}
      style={mobile
        ? { top: visualViewport?.top ?? 0, left: 0, right: 0, height: visualViewport?.height ?? "100dvh" }
        : { bottom: "calc(5rem + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))", right: "1.25rem" }}
      className={`pointer-events-auto fixed z-[71] overflow-hidden bg-white shadow-2xl outline-none ${mobile ? "w-full" : "h-[min(680px,calc(100dvh-7rem))] w-[min(420px,calc(100vw-2.5rem))] rounded-2xl border border-[#17251D]/15"} ${isOpen ? "block" : "hidden"}`}
      onMouseDown={(event) => {
        if (mobile && event.target === event.currentTarget) changeOpen(false);
      }}
    >
      <Conversation
        lotId={lotId}
        title={title}
        embedded
        presentation="floating"
        onMinimize={() => changeOpen(false)}
        onClose={() => changeOpen(false)}
      />
    </div>
    {!isOpen && <button
      type="button"
      aria-label={lotId ? `Abrir asistente para el lote ${lotId}` : "Abrir asistente operativo"}
      aria-expanded={false}
      onClick={(event) => {
        opener.current = event.currentTarget;
        changeOpen(true);
      }}
      style={{ bottom: "calc(1.25rem + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))", right: "calc(1.25rem + env(safe-area-inset-right))" }}
      className="pointer-events-auto fixed z-[71] flex min-h-12 items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-[#1B4332] px-4 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-lg outline-none transition hover:bg-[#2D6A4F] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#2D6A4F] focus-visible:ring-offset-2"
    >
      <MessageCircle size={18} aria-hidden="true" /> Asistente IA
      {lotId && <span className="max-w-28 truncate text-xs font-medium text-white/80">{lotId}</span>}
    </button>}
  </div>, document.body);
}

function Conversation({
  lotId,
  title,
  embedded,
  presentation,
  onMinimize,
  onClose,
}: {
  lotId: string | null;
  title: string;
  embedded: boolean;
  presentation: Presentation;
  onMinimize?: () => void;
  onClose?: () => void;
}) {
  const inputId = useId();
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([]);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [failureKind, setFailureKind] = useState<AssistantFailureKind | null>(null);
  const [receivedResponse, setReceivedResponse] = useState(false);
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [hasNewMessages, setHasNewMessages] = useState(false);
  const sending = useRef(false);
  const alive = useRef(false);
  const activeRequest = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const list = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const stick = useRef(true);
  const previousMessageCount = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    alive.current = true;
    return () => {
      alive.current = false;
      activeRequest.current?.abort();
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (stick.current && list.current) {
      list.current.scrollTop = list.current.scrollHeight;
      setHasNewMessages(false);
    } else if (messages.length > previousMessageCount.current) {
      setHasNewMessages(true);
    }
    previousMessageCount.current = messages.length;
  }, [messages, busy]);

  async function submit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const question = draft.trim();
    if (!question || question.length > 1000 || sending.current) return;
    sending.current = true;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    activeRequest.current = controller;
    setBusy(true);
    setError("");
    setFailureKind(null);
    stick.current = true;
    setMessages((items) => {
      const last = items[items.length - 1];
      return last?.role === "user" && last.text === question ? items : [...items, { role: "user", text: question }];
    });
    setDraft("");
    try {
      const answer = await askGemini(question, lotId, controller.signal);
      if (!alive.current) return;
      setReceivedResponse(true);
      setMessages((items) => [...items, { role: "assistant", text: answer }]);
    } catch (cause) {
      if (!alive.current || controller.signal.aborted) return;
      setReceivedResponse(false);
      setFailureKind(cause instanceof AssistantRequestError ? cause.kind : "unavailable");
      setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "No se pudo completar la consulta.");
      setDraft(question);
    } finally {
      if (activeRequest.current === controller) activeRequest.current = null;
      sending.current = false;
      if (alive.current) setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  function keyDown(event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) {
    if (event.key === "Enter" && !event.shiftKey && !event.nativeEvent.isComposing && event.nativeEvent.keyCode !== 229) {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.currentTarget.form?.requestSubmit();
    }
  }

  const serviceStatus = busy
    ? "Consultando el servicio configurado…"
    : receivedResponse
      ? "El servicio devolvió una respuesta en esta sesión."
      : failureKind === "session"
        ? "La sesión o el acceso no fueron aceptados."
        : failureKind === "quota"
          ? "Se alcanzó un límite de consultas."
          : failureKind === "configuration"
            ? "El servicio necesita revisión de configuración."
            : failureKind === "request"
              ? "Revisa la consulta y el lote seleccionado."
              : failureKind === "unavailable"
                ? "No se recibió una respuesta del servicio."
                : "La conexión se comprobará al enviar una consulta.";

  return <section className={`flex h-full min-h-0 min-w-0 flex-col overflow-hidden border border-[#1B4332]/15 bg-white ${presentation === "floating" ? "w-full rounded-none sm:rounded-2xl" : `rounded-2xl shadow-sm ${embedded ? "h-full min-h-0 max-h-full w-full" : "min-h-[420px] max-h-[calc(100dvh-8rem)] self-start lg:sticky lg:top-6 lg:max-h-[calc(100vh-8rem)]"}`}`} aria-label={title}>
    <header className="shrink-0 border-b border-[#1B4332]/10 bg-[#F5F8F4] p-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3"><span className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#B7D96B] text-[#17251D]"><Bot size={20} /></span><h2 className="truncate text-lg font-bold text-[#17251D]">{title}</h2></div>
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1">
          {presentation === "floating" ? <>
            <button type="button" onClick={onMinimize} aria-label="Minimizar asistente" className="grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-lg text-[#526157] outline-none hover:bg-white focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#2D6A4F]"><Minimize2 size={17} /></button>
            <button type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Cerrar asistente" className="grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-lg text-[#526157] outline-none hover:bg-white focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#2D6A4F]"><X size={18} /></button>
          </> : <button type="button" onClick={() => setCollapsed((value) => !value)} className="rounded-lg border border-[#1B4332]/10 bg-white px-2 py-2 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[.12em] text-[#1B4332]" aria-expanded={!collapsed}>{collapsed ? "Abrir" : "Minimizar"}</button>}
        </div>
      </div>
      {!collapsed && <>
        <p role="status" className="mt-3 text-xs font-semibold text-[#2D6A4F]">{serviceStatus}</p>
        <p className="mt-2 break-words text-xs leading-5 text-[#526157]">{lotId ? `Lote seleccionado: ${lotId}. El servidor comprobará tu acceso.` : "Consulta general con el resumen operativo autorizado. Selecciona un lote para consultar sus detalles."}</p>
      </>}
    </header>
    {!collapsed && <>
      <div
        ref={list}
        role="log"
        aria-live="polite"
        aria-label="Conversación con el asistente"
        onScroll={(event) => {
          const el = event.currentTarget;
          stick.current = el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight < 48;
          if (stick.current) setHasNewMessages(false);
        }}
        className="relative min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain p-4"
      >
        {!messages.length && <p className="rounded-xl bg-[#F5F8F4] p-3 text-sm leading-6 text-[#17251D]">Consulta sobre trazabilidad, documentación, el resumen operativo o el lote seleccionado. Cada pregunta se evalúa con el contexto actual; no se envía el historial de la conversación.</p>}
        {messages.map((message, index) => <div key={index} className={`flex ${message.role === "user" ? "justify-end" : "justify-start"}`}><p className={`max-w-[94%] whitespace-pre-wrap break-words rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-6 ${message.role === "user" ? "rounded-tr-sm bg-[#B7D96B] text-[#17251D]" : "rounded-tl-sm border border-[#1B4332]/10 bg-[#F5F8F4] text-[#17251D]"}`}><span className="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold">{message.role === "user" ? "Tú" : "Asistente · Gemini"}</span>{message.text}</p></div>)}
        {busy && <p role="status" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-[#17251D]"><LoaderCircle size={16} className="animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none" /> Preparando respuesta…</p>}
        {hasNewMessages && <div className="sticky bottom-1 flex justify-end"><button type="button" onClick={() => {
          stick.current = true;
          if (list.current) list.current.scrollTop = list.current.scrollHeight;
          setHasNewMessages(false);
        }} className="inline-flex min-h-9 items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-[#1B4332] px-3 text-xs font-semibold text-white shadow outline-none hover:bg-[#2D6A4F] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#2D6A4F] focus-visible:ring-offset-2"><ChevronDown size={15} /> Mensajes nuevos</button></div>}
      </div>
      <form onSubmit={(event) => void submit(event)} className="shrink-0 border-t border-[#1B4332]/10 bg-white p-3 pb-[max(0.75rem,env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] sm:p-4">
        {error && <p role="alert" className="mb-3 rounded-xl bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-800">{error} Puedes corregir o reintentar la consulta; tu texto se conserva.</p>}
        <label htmlFor={inputId} className="sr-only">Tu consulta</label>
        <div className="flex items-end gap-2"><textarea id={inputId} value={draft} onChange={(event) => setDraft(event.target.value)} onKeyDown={keyDown} rows={2} maxLength={1000} disabled={busy} placeholder="Escribe tu consulta…" className="max-h-32 min-h-12 min-w-0 flex-1 resize-y rounded-xl border border-[#526157]/30 bg-field p-3 text-sm text-[#17251D] outline-none placeholder:text-[#526157] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F] disabled:bg-[#F5F8F4]" /><button type="submit" disabled={busy || !draft.trim()} aria-label="Enviar consulta" className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#1B4332] text-white outline-none hover:bg-[#2D6A4F] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#2D6A4F] focus-visible:ring-offset-2 disabled:bg-[#E8EEE7] disabled:text-[#526157]"><Send size={18} /></button></div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] leading-5 text-[#526157]">Enter envía · Shift+Enter agrega línea. La IA no consulta normativa vigente ni autoriza aplicaciones o despachos. No incluyas datos personales.</p>
      </form>
    </>}
  </section>;
}

==> src/features/assistant/assistant-api.ts <==
import { getSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";

export type AssistantFailureKind = "session" | "quota" | "configuration" | "request" | "unavailable";

export class AssistantRequestError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string, readonly kind: AssistantFailureKind) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "AssistantRequestError";
  }
}

export function savedLotId(value?: string | null): string | null {
  return value && /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(value) ? value : null;
}

export async function askGemini(question: string, lotId: string | null, signal?: AbortSignal) {
  const client = getSupabaseBrowserClient();
  if (!client) throw new AssistantRequestError("Supabase no está configurado en esta aplicación.", "configuration");
  const { data, error } = await client.functions.invoke("asistente-ia", {
    body: { question, lotId },
    signal,
  });
  if (error) {
    let message = "No se recibió una respuesta del asistente. Revisa tu conexión e intenta de nuevo.";
    let kind: AssistantFailureKind = "unavailable";
    // Function HTTP errors expose a Response in context. Never render a raw stack or token.
    const context: unknown = error.context;
    if (context instanceof Response) {
      if (context.status === 401 || context.status === 403) kind = "session";
      else if (context.status === 429) kind = "quota";
      else if (context.status >= 400 && context.status < 500) kind = "request";
      try {
        const body = await context.clone().json();
        if (body && typeof body === "object" && "error" in body && typeof body.error === "string" && body.error.length <= 300) {
          message = body.error;
          if (/configur|migraci[oó]n|cuota del asistente|modelo configurado|permisos/i.test(message)) kind = "configuration";
        }
      } catch { /* Keep the friendly network error. */ }
    }
    throw new AssistantRequestError(message, kind);
  }
  if (!data || data.provider !== "gemini" || typeof data.answer !== "string") {
    throw new AssistantRequestError("El asistente devolvió una respuesta inválida.", "unavailable");
  }
  return data.answer;
}

==> src/features/auth/AuthGate.tsx <==
"use client";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useAuth } from "./AuthProvider";
import { roleHome, routeRole } from "./roles";

export function SessionStatus({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-stone-50 p-6"><section className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-green-900/10 bg-white p-8 shadow-sm">{children}</section></main>;
}
export function AuthGate({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const { client, session, profile, loading, error, reload, signOut, recovery } = useAuth();
  const path = usePathname(); const router = useRouter();
  const expected = routeRole(path);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (loading) return;
    if (!session || recovery) router.replace("/login");
    else if (profile?.is_active && expected && profile.rol !== expected) router.replace(roleHome[profile.rol]);
  }, [loading, session, recovery, profile, expected, router]);
  if (!client) return <SessionStatus><h1 className="text-xl font-bold">Configura el Supabase de Agrícola Don Ricardo</h1><p className="mt-3 text-sm text-gray-600">Faltan las variables públicas del proyecto independiente de IA.</p></SessionStatus>;
  if (loading || !session || recovery) return <SessionStatus><p role="status">Comprobando tu acceso...</p></SessionStatus>;
  if (error || !profile || !profile.is_active) return <SessionStatus><h1 className="text-xl font-bold">{profile && !profile.is_active ? "Cuenta pendiente de habilitación" : "Perfil no disponible"}</h1><p className="mt-3 text-sm text-gray-600">{error || "Gerencia debe habilitar tu cuenta y asignarte un área antes de operar."}</p><div className="mt-6 flex gap-4"><button className="font-bold text-green-900" onClick={reload}>Revisar acceso</button><button onClick={() => void signOut().catch(() => alert("No se pudo cerrar sesión. Inténtalo nuevamente."))}>Cerrar sesión</button></div></SessionStatus>;
  if (expected && profile.rol !== expected) return <SessionStatus><p role="status">Abriendo tu área de trabajo...</p></SessionStatus>;
  return children;
}

==> src/features/auth/AuthProvider.tsx <==
"use client";
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { Session } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { getSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";
import { useTraceabilityStore } from "@/store/useTraceabilityStore";
import { isUserRole, type UserProfile } from "./roles";

type AuthContextValue = {
  client: ReturnType<typeof getSupabaseBrowserClient>;
  session: Session | null; profile: UserProfile | null; loading: boolean; error: string;
  recovery: boolean; clearRecovery: () => void; reload: () => void;
  updateProfile: (updates: Partial<Pick<UserProfile, "nombre" | "telefono" | "descripcion" | "avatar_path">>) => void;
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
};
const Context = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);
export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const client = useMemo(getSupabaseBrowserClient, []);
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<UserProfile | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(Boolean(client));
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [recovery, setRecovery] = useState(false);
  const [retry, setRetry] = useState(0);
  const generation = useRef(0);
  const signOut = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!client) return;
    const { error: requestError } = await client.auth.signOut();
    if (requestError) throw requestError;
    useTraceabilityStore.getState().clearStore();
    localStorage.removeItem("nativa-auth");
    document.cookie = "nativa-role=; Path=/; Max-Age=0; SameSite=Lax";
    setRecovery(false);
  }, [client]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!client) return;
    let active = true;
    let currentUser: string | null = null;
    let authEvents = 0;
    async function loadProfile(nextSession: Session | null, force = false) {
      if (!active) return;
      setSession(nextSession);
      const id = nextSession?.user.id ?? null;
      if (!force && id && id === currentUser) return;
      currentUser = id;
      const request = ++generation.current;
      setProfile(null); setError("");
      useTraceabilityStore.getState().clearStore();
      if (!id) { setLoading(false); return; }
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const { data, error: requestError } = await client!.from("usuarios").select("id, nombre, email, rol, is_active, telefono, descripcion, avatar_path").eq("id", id).maybeSingle();
        if (!active || request !== generation.current) return;
        if (requestError) throw new Error(requestError.message);
        if (!data || !isUserRole(data.rol)) throw new Error("Tu cuenta aún no tiene un perfil válido. Contacta a Gerencia.");
        setProfile({
          id: data.id,
          nombre: data.nombre,
          email: data.email,
          rol: data.rol,
          is_active: data.is_active,
          telefono: data.telefono,
          descripcion: data.descripcion,
          avatar_path: data.avatar_path,
        });
      } catch (cause) {
        if (active && request === generation.current) setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "No se pudo cargar tu perfil.");
      } finally { if (active && request === generation.current) setLoading(false); }
    }
    const { data: listener } = client.auth.onAuthStateChange((event, nextSession) => {
      if (!active) return;
      authEvents += 1;
      if (event === "PASSWORD_RECOVERY") setRecovery(true);
      // Supabase callbacks must return before making another Auth request.
      setTimeout(() => { if (active) void loadProfile(nextSession); }, 0);
    });
    const startupEvents = authEvents;
    void client.auth.getSession().then(({ data, error: sessionError }) => {
      if (!active || authEvents !== startupEvents) return;
      if (sessionError) { setError(sessionError.message); setLoading(false); return; }
      void loadProfile(data.session, true);
    }).catch(() => { if (active) { setError("No se pudo restaurar la sesión."); setLoading(false); } });
    return () => { active = false; listener.subscription.unsubscribe(); };
  }, [client, retry]);
  const updateProfile = useCallback((updates: Partial<Pick<UserProfile, "nombre" | "telefono" | "descripcion" | "avatar_path">>) => {
    setProfile((current) => current ? { ...current, ...updates } : current);
  }, []);
  const value = { client, session, profile, loading, error, recovery, clearRecovery: () => setRecovery(false), reload: () => setRetry((value) => value + 1), updateProfile, signOut };
  return <Context.Provider value={value}>{children}</Context.Provider>;
}
export function useAuth() { const context = useContext(Context); if (!context) throw new Error("AuthProvider no está montado."); return context; }

==> src/features/auth/roles.ts <==
export type UserRole = "ADMIN" | "SUPERVISOR" | "OPERADOR";
export type UserProfile = {
  id: string;
  nombre: string;
  email: string;
  rol: UserRole;
  is_active: boolean;
  telefono: string | null;
  descripcion: string | null;
  avatar_path: string | null;
};
export const roleHome: Record<UserRole, "/gerente" | "/packing" | "/campo"> = { ADMIN: "/gerente", SUPERVISOR: "/packing", OPERADOR: "/campo" };
export const roleLabels: Record<UserRole, string> = { ADMIN: "Gerencia", SUPERVISOR: "Supervisor de Packing", OPERADOR: "Operador de Campo" };
export const roleAreas: Record<UserRole, string> = { ADMIN: "Gerencia", SUPERVISOR: "Packing", OPERADOR: "Campo" };
export function isUserRole(value: unknown): value is UserRole { return value === "ADMIN" || value === "SUPERVISOR" || value === "OPERADOR"; }
export function routeRole(path: string): UserRole | null {
  return path === "/gerente" || path === "/gerente/" ? "ADMIN" : path === "/packing" || path === "/packing/" ? "SUPERVISOR" : path === "/campo" || path === "/campo/" ? "OPERADOR" : null;
}

"use client";

import type { ReactNode } from "react";

export const chartColors = {
  forest: "var(--chart-primary)",
  green: "var(--chart-green)",
  sage: "var(--chart-sage)",
  lime: "var(--accent-soft)",

  process: "var(--chart-process)",
  muted: "var(--chart-axis)",
};

export type LegendItem = { label: string; color: string; value?: string | number };

export function DashboardMetric({
  label,
  value,
  unit,
  detail,
  icon: Icon,
  comparison,
  onClick,
  tone,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string | number;
  unit?: string;
  detail: string;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  comparison?: string;
  onClick?: () => void;
  tone?: "approved";
}) {
  const content = <CardContent className="p-4 sm:p-5">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-secondary">{label}</p>
              <p className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-1.5 text-3xl font-bold tabular-nums text-primary"><span>{value}</span>{unit && <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-secondary">{unit}</span>}</p>
        </div>
        <span className={`flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl ${tone === "approved" ? "bg-[#1B4332] text-white" : "bg-[#E8EEE7] text-[#1B4332]"}`}><Icon size={18} /></span>
      </div>
      <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-secondary">{detail}</p>
      {comparison && <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-semibold text-secondary">{comparison}</p>}
      {onClick && <button type="button" onClick={onClick} className="absolute inset-0 rounded-2xl outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-[#2D6A4F]" aria-label={`${label}: ${value}. Filtrar lotes relacionados.`} />}
    </CardContent>
  return <Card className={`relative h-full rounded-2xl ${onClick ? "transition hover:border-[#2D6A4F]/40 hover:shadow-md" : ""}`}>
    {content}
  </Card>;
}

export function ChartCard({ title, description, children }: { title: string; description: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <Card className="min-w-0 rounded-2xl">
  <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle><p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-secondary">{description}</p></CardHeader>
    <CardContent className="min-w-0">{children}</CardContent>
  </Card>;
}

export function ChartLoading({ label = "Cargando datos del gráfico…" }: { label?: string }) {

  return <div role="alert" className="flex min-h-[180px] flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 px-5 text-center text-sm text-red-900"><AlertCircle size={20} /><p>{message}</p><Button type="button" variant="outline" className="min-h-9" onClick={onRetry}><RefreshCw size={15} /> Reintentar</Button></div>;
}

export function DashboardLegend({ items }: { items: LegendItem[] }) {
  return <ul className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-2" aria-label="Leyenda del gráfico">

  </ul>;
}

export function DashboardTooltip({
  active,
  payload,
  label,
}: Partial<TooltipContentProps<number | string, string>>) {
  if (!active || !payload?.length) return null;
  const row = payload[0].payload as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  const rowHeading = row?.fullLabel ?? row?.label ?? row?.name ?? label ?? "";
  const heading = String(rowHeading);
  return <div className="rounded-lg border border-default bg-surface-raised px-3 py-2 shadow-lg">
    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-secondary">{heading}</p>
    <div className="mt-1 space-y-1">{payload.map((entry, index) => <p key={`${String(entry.dataKey ?? entry.name)}-${index}`} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-primary"><span aria-hidden="true" className="h-2 w-2 rounded-sm" style={{ backgroundColor: entry.color ?? chartColors.green }} /><span>{String(entry.name ?? entry.dataKey ?? "Registros")}:</span><strong className="tabular-nums">{Number(entry.value ?? 0).toLocaleString("es-PE")}</strong></p>)}</div>
  </div>;
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
