"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { ArrowDownWideNarrow, FileUp, LoaderCircle, Search, Send } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { useAuth } from "@/features/auth/AuthProvider";
import type { Destination } from "@/store/useTraceabilityStore";

type DispatchHistory = {
  codigo: string;
  lote_id: string;
  destino: Destination;
  cajas: number;
  estado: "PENDIENTE_FIRMA" | "APROBADO" | "RECHAZADO";
  created_at: string;
  motivo_revision: string | null;
};

const allowedTypes = new Set(["application/pdf", "image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"]);
const maxFileSize = 10 * 1024 * 1024;
const statusLabels: Record<DispatchHistory["estado"], string> = {
  PENDIENTE_FIRMA: "Pendiente de revisión",
  APROBADO: "Aprobado y liberado",
  RECHAZADO: "Requiere corrección",
};

function fileMimeType(file: File) {
  if (file.type) return file.type;
  const extension = file.name.split(".").pop()?.toLowerCase();
  return extension === "pdf" ? "application/pdf"
    : extension === "jpg" || extension === "jpeg" ? "image/jpeg"
      : extension === "png" ? "image/png"
        : extension === "webp" ? "image/webp" : "";
}

export function PackingDispatchSubmission({
  lotId,
  destination,
  boxes,
  eligible,
  received,
}: {
  lotId: string;
  destination: Destination;
  boxes: number;
  eligible: boolean;
  received: boolean;
}) {
  const { client, profile } = useAuth();
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<DispatchHistory[]>([]);
  const [historyQuery, setHistoryQuery] = useState("");
  const [historyStatus, setHistoryStatus] = useState<DispatchHistory["estado"] | "ALL">("ALL");
  const [historyDateOrder, setHistoryDateOrder] = useState<"newest" | "oldest">("newest");

  const loadHistory = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!client || !profile?.id) return;
    const { data, error: requestError } = await client
      .from("despachos")
      .select("codigo,lote_id,destino,cajas,estado,created_at,motivo_revision")
      .eq("creado_por", profile.id)
      .order("created_at", { ascending: false });
    if (requestError) {
      setError(`No se pudo consultar el historial de despachos: ${requestError.message}`);
      return;
    }
    setHistory((data ?? []) as DispatchHistory[]);
  }, [client, profile?.id]);

  useEffect(() => { void loadHistory(); }, [loadHistory]);

  const visibleHistory = useMemo(() => {
    const normalizedQuery = historyQuery.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("es");
    return history
      .filter((item) => (!normalizedQuery || [item.codigo, item.lote_id, item.destino]
        .some((value) => value.toLocaleLowerCase("es").includes(normalizedQuery)))
        && (historyStatus === "ALL" || item.estado === historyStatus))
      .sort((left, right) => {
        const difference = Date.parse(left.created_at) - Date.parse(right.created_at);
        return historyDateOrder === "newest" ? -difference : difference;
      });
  }, [history, historyQuery, historyStatus, historyDateOrder]);

  async function submit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    if (!client || !profile?.id) {
      setError("No está disponible la sesión activa de Packing.");
      return;
    }
    if (!received) {
      setError("Primero registra la recepción del lote desde la sección Recepción.");
      return;
    }
    if (!eligible) {
      setError("El lote todavía no cumple los requisitos del destino seleccionado.");
      return;
    }
    if (!Number.isInteger(boxes) || boxes < 1 || boxes > 1000000) {
      setError("Ingresa una cantidad válida de cajas.");
      return;
    }
    if (!file) {
      setError("Adjunta el informe o ficha del despacho antes de enviarlo.");
      return;
    }
    const mimeType = fileMimeType(file);
    if (!allowedTypes.has(mimeType) || file.size < 1 || file.size > maxFileSize) {
      setError("Adjunta PDF, JPG, PNG o WebP de hasta 10 MB.");
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    const requestId = crypto.randomUUID();
    const safeName = file.name.replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}._-]/gu, "_").slice(-180) || "informe";
    const storagePath = `${profile.id}/${requestId}/${safeName}`;
    let uploaded = false;
    try {
      const { error: uploadError } = await client.storage
        .from("despacho-documentos")
        .upload(storagePath, file, { contentType: mimeType, upsert: false });
      if (uploadError) throw new Error(`No se pudo subir el informe: ${uploadError.message}`);
      uploaded = true;

      const { data, error: createError } = await client.rpc("crear_despacho", {
        p_lote_id: lotId,
        p_destino: destination,
        p_cajas: boxes,
        p_storage_path: storagePath,
        p_nombre_archivo: file.name.slice(0, 180),
        p_mime_type: mimeType,
        p_size_bytes: file.size,
      });
      if (createError) throw new Error(createError.message);

      const created = data as { codigo?: string } | null;
      setNotice(`Despacho ${created?.codigo ?? "registrado"} enviado a Gerencia con el informe adjunto.`);
      setFile(null);
      if (inputRef.current) inputRef.current.value = "";
      await loadHistory();
    } catch (cause) {
      if (uploaded) {
        await client.storage.from("despacho-documentos").remove([storagePath]);
      }
      setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "No se pudo enviar el despacho.");
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return <div className="space-y-5">
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2"><FileUp size={19} className="text-[#1B4332]" /> Enviar ficha a Gerencia</CardTitle>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">Adjunta el informe del despacho. El expediente quedará pendiente de revisión administrativa.</p>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <form onSubmit={(event) => void submit(event)} className="space-y-4">
          <div className="grid gap-3 border border-gray-100 bg-[#F5F8F4] p-4 text-sm sm:grid-cols-3">
            <p><span className="block text-xs text-gray-500">Lote</span><strong className="mt-1 block break-all">{lotId}</strong></p>
            <p><span className="block text-xs text-gray-500">Destino</span><strong className="mt-1 block">{destination}</strong></p>
            <p><span className="block text-xs text-gray-500">Cajas</span><strong className="mt-1 block">{Number.isInteger(boxes) ? boxes.toLocaleString("es-PE") : "Pendiente"}</strong></p>
          </div>
          <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D]">
            Informe o ficha de despacho
            <input ref={inputRef} type="file" accept=".pdf,.jpg,.jpeg,.png,.webp,application/pdf,image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp" onChange={(event) => setFile(event.target.files?.[0] ?? null)} className="mt-2 block min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-2 text-sm file:mr-3 file:border-0 file:bg-[#E8EEE7] file:px-3 file:py-2 file:font-semibold file:text-[#1B4332]" />
            <span className="mt-1 block text-xs font-normal text-gray-500">PDF, JPG, PNG o WebP · máximo 10 MB · almacenado de forma privada.</span>
          </label>
          {error && <p role="alert" className="border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-800">{error}</p>}
          {notice && <p role="status" className="border border-green-200 bg-green-50 p-3 text-sm text-green-900">{notice}</p>}
          <Button type="submit" disabled={submitting || !received || !eligible || !Number.isInteger(boxes) || boxes < 1}>
            {submitting ? <LoaderCircle size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <Send size={16} />}
            {submitting ? "Subiendo y enviando…" : !received ? "Registra primero la recepción" : !eligible ? "Bloqueado por validación del lote" : "Subir informe y enviar a Gerencia"}
          </Button>
          {!received && <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-amber-800">El lote debe estar registrado como recibido por Packing antes de iniciar el despacho.</p>}
        </form>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>

    <Card>
      <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="text-base">Historial de despachos</CardTitle><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">Estado persistente de los informes enviados por tu cuenta.</p></CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-2">
        {history.length === 0 ? <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Todavía no hay despachos enviados desde esta cuenta.</p> : <>
          <div className="grid gap-3 border-b border-gray-100 pb-3 sm:grid-cols-[minmax(220px,1fr)_190px_200px]">
            <label className="relative block"><span className="sr-only">Buscar mis despachos</span><Search size={15} aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-500" /><input type="search" value={historyQuery} onChange={(event) => setHistoryQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Buscar código o lote…" className="min-h-10 w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-200 pl-9 pr-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20" /></label>
            <label><span className="sr-only">Filtrar historial por estado</span><select value={historyStatus} onChange={(event) => setHistoryStatus(event.target.value as DispatchHistory["estado"] | "ALL")} className="min-h-10 w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white px-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20"><option value="ALL">Todos los estados</option><option value="PENDIENTE_FIRMA">Pendientes</option><option value="APROBADO">Aprobados</option><option value="RECHAZADO">Devueltos</option></select></label>
            <label className="relative"><span className="sr-only">Ordenar historial por fecha</span><ArrowDownWideNarrow size={15} aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-500" /><select value={historyDateOrder} onChange={(event) => setHistoryDateOrder(event.target.value as "newest" | "oldest")} className="min-h-10 w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white pl-9 pr-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20"><option value="newest">Más recientes primero</option><option value="oldest">Más antiguos primero</option></select></label>
          </div>
          {visibleHistory.length === 0 ? <p className="py-5 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">No hay envíos que coincidan con esos filtros.</p> : visibleHistory.map((item) => (
          <div key={item.codigo} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-gray-100 py-3 text-sm">
            <div><p className="font-bold text-[#17251D]">{item.codigo} · {item.destino} · {item.cajas.toLocaleString("es-PE")} cajas</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">Lote {item.lote_id} · {new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleDateString("es-PE")}</p>{item.motivo_revision && <p className="mt-1 max-w-xl text-xs text-red-800">Observación: {item.motivo_revision}</p>}</div>
            <span className={`px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${item.estado === "APROBADO" ? "bg-green-100 text-green-800" : item.estado === "RECHAZADO" ? "bg-red-100 text-red-800" : "bg-amber-100 text-amber-900"}`}>{statusLabels[item.estado]}</span>
          </div>
          ))}
        </>}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  </div>;
}
==> src/features/dispatches/SupervisionDespachos.tsx <==
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { AlertCircle, ArrowDownWideNarrow, Check, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Clock3, FileCheck2, FileText, Package, RefreshCw, Search, ShieldCheck, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { useAuth } from "@/features/auth/AuthProvider";

type DispatchState = "PENDIENTE_FIRMA" | "APROBADO" | "RECHAZADO";
type OperationStatus = "EN_CAMPO" | "EN_PACKING" | "PENDIENTE_FIRMA" | "APROBADO" | "RECHAZADO";
type OperationLot = { id: string; fundo: string; cultivo: string; estado: OperationStatus; created_at: string };
type DispatchDocument = { storage_path: string; nombre_archivo: string; mime_type: string; size_bytes: number };
type DispatchRow = {
  id: string;
  codigo: string;
  lote_id: string;
  destino: string;
  cajas: number;
  estado: DispatchState;
  created_at: string;
  revisado_at: string | null;
  motivo_revision: string | null;
  lot?: { fundo: string; cultivo: string };
  document?: DispatchDocument;
};

const stateLabels: Record<DispatchState, string> = {
  PENDIENTE_FIRMA: "Pendiente de revisión",
  APROBADO: "Aprobado y liberado",
  RECHAZADO: "Requiere corrección",
};
const operationStatusLabels: Record<OperationStatus, string> = {
  EN_CAMPO: "En campo",
  EN_PACKING: "En Packing",
  PENDIENTE_FIRMA: "Pendiente de firma",
  APROBADO: "Aprobado",
  RECHAZADO: "Rechazado",
};
const operationStatusColors: Record<OperationStatus, string> = {
  EN_CAMPO: "#2D6A4F",
  EN_PACKING: "#4E91A3",
  PENDIENTE_FIRMA: "#D79A27",
  APROBADO: "#6E9F46",
  RECHAZADO: "#C9603F",
};
const operationStates = Object.keys(operationStatusLabels) as OperationStatus[];

export function SupervisionDespachos() {
  const { client } = useAuth();
  const [dispatches, setDispatches] = useState<DispatchRow[]>([]);
  const [operationLots, setOperationLots] = useState<OperationLot[]>([]);
  const [operationError, setOperationError] = useState("");
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [reviewNote, setReviewNote] = useState("");
  const [signedUrl, setSignedUrl] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<DispatchState | "ALL">("ALL");
  const [dateOrder, setDateOrder] = useState<"newest" | "oldest">("newest");
  const [operationQuery, setOperationQuery] = useState("");
  const [operationStatus, setOperationStatus] = useState<OperationStatus | "ALL">("ALL");
  const [operationDateOrder, setOperationDateOrder] = useState<"newest" | "oldest">("newest");
  const [operationPage, setOperationPage] = useState(1);
  const selected = dispatches.find((item) => item.id === selectedId) ?? null;

  const loadDispatches = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!client) {
      setError("No está disponible la conexión con Supabase.");
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    setOperationError("");
    const [dispatchResult, operationResult] = await Promise.all([
      client.from("despachos")
        .select("id,codigo,lote_id,destino,cajas,estado,created_at,revisado_at,motivo_revision")
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false }),
      client.from("lotes")
        .select("id,fundo,cultivo,estado,created_at")
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false }),
    ]);
    const { data, error: dispatchError } = dispatchResult;
    if (operationResult.error) {
      setOperationLots([]);
      setOperationError(`No se pudieron cargar los lotes de operación: ${operationResult.error.message}`);
    } else {
      setOperationLots((operationResult.data ?? []) as OperationLot[]);
    }
    if (dispatchError) {
      setError(`No se pudieron cargar los despachos: ${dispatchError.message}`);
      setDispatches([]);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const rows = (data ?? []) as Omit<DispatchRow, "lot" | "document">[];
    const ids = [...new Set(rows.map((item) => item.lote_id))];
    const dispatchIds = rows.map((item) => item.id);
    const [lotResult, documentResult] = ids.length ? await Promise.all([
      client.from("lotes").select("id,fundo,cultivo").in("id", ids),
      client.from("despacho_documentos").select("despacho_id,storage_path,nombre_archivo,mime_type,size_bytes").in("despacho_id", dispatchIds),
    ]) : [{ data: [], error: null }, { data: [], error: null }];

    if (lotResult.error || documentResult.error) {
      setError(`Se cargó la lista, pero faltan datos relacionados: ${lotResult.error?.message ?? documentResult.error?.message}`);
      setDispatches(rows);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    const lots = new Map((lotResult.data ?? []).map((lot) => [lot.id, lot]));
    const documents = new Map((documentResult.data ?? []).map((document) => [document.despacho_id, document]));
    setDispatches(rows.map((item) => ({
      ...item,
      lot: lots.get(item.lote_id),
      document: documents.get(item.id),
    })));
    setLoading(false);
  }, [client]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadDispatches();
    if (!client) return;
    const channel = client.channel("despachos-supervision")
      .on("postgres_changes", { event: "*", schema: "public", table: "despachos" }, () => { void loadDispatches(); })
      .subscribe();
    return () => { void client.removeChannel(channel); };
  }, [client, loadDispatches]);

  const counts = useMemo(() => ({
    total: dispatches.length,
    pending: dispatches.filter((item) => item.estado === "PENDIENTE_FIRMA").length,
    approved: dispatches.filter((item) => item.estado === "APROBADO").length,
    rejected: dispatches.filter((item) => item.estado === "RECHAZADO").length,
  }), [dispatches]);
  const operationCounts = useMemo(() => operationStates.map((estado) => ({
    estado,
    total: operationLots.filter((lot) => lot.estado === estado).length,
  })), [operationLots]);
  const operationTotal = operationLots.length;
  let chartAngle = 0;
  const pieSegments = operationCounts.map(({ estado, total }) => {
    const start = chartAngle;
    chartAngle += operationTotal ? (total / operationTotal) * 360 : 0;
    return `${operationStatusColors[estado]} ${start}deg ${chartAngle}deg`;
  });
  const pieBackground = operationTotal
    ? `conic-gradient(${pieSegments.join(", ")})`
    : "conic-gradient(#E8EEE7 0deg 360deg)";

  const visibleDispatches = useMemo(() => {
    const normalizedQuery = query.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("es");
    return dispatches
      .filter((item) => {
        const searchable = [item.codigo, item.lote_id, item.destino, item.lot?.fundo, item.lot?.cultivo]
          .some((value) => value?.toLocaleLowerCase("es").includes(normalizedQuery));
        return (!normalizedQuery || searchable) && (statusFilter === "ALL" || item.estado === statusFilter);
      })
      .sort((left, right) => {
        const difference = Date.parse(left.created_at) - Date.parse(right.created_at);
        return dateOrder === "newest" ? -difference : difference;
      });
  }, [dispatches, query, statusFilter, dateOrder]);

  const visibleOperationLots = useMemo(() => {
    const normalizedQuery = operationQuery.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("es");
    return operationLots.filter((lot) => {
      const matchesQuery = !normalizedQuery || [lot.id, lot.fundo, lot.cultivo, operationStatusLabels[lot.estado]]
        .some((value) => value.toLocaleLowerCase("es").includes(normalizedQuery));
      return matchesQuery && (operationStatus === "ALL" || lot.estado === operationStatus);
    }).sort((left, right) => {
      const difference = Date.parse(left.created_at) - Date.parse(right.created_at);
      return operationDateOrder === "newest" ? -difference : difference;
    });
  }, [operationLots, operationQuery, operationStatus, operationDateOrder]);
  const operationPageSize = 10;
  const operationPageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(visibleOperationLots.length / operationPageSize));
  const currentOperationPage = Math.min(operationPage, operationPageCount);
  const pagedOperationLots = visibleOperationLots.slice(
    (currentOperationPage - 1) * operationPageSize,
    currentOperationPage * operationPageSize,
  );

  useEffect(() => { setOperationPage(1); }, [operationQuery, operationStatus, operationDateOrder]);

  async function createDocumentLink(item: DispatchRow) {
    if (!client || !item.document) return;
    setError("");
    const { data, error: urlError } = await client.storage.from("despacho-documentos").createSignedUrl(item.document.storage_path, 120);
    if (urlError) {
      setError(`No se pudo preparar el acceso privado al informe: ${urlError.message}`);
      return;
    }
    setSignedUrl(data.signedUrl);
  }

  async function resolve(approve: boolean) {
    if (!client || !selected) return;
    if (!approve && reviewNote.trim().length < 5) {
      setError("Escribe un motivo de al menos 5 caracteres para devolver el expediente.");
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    const { error: requestError } = await client.rpc("resolver_despacho", {
      p_despacho_id: selected.id,
      p_aprobar: approve,
      p_motivo: reviewNote.trim() || null,
    });
    if (requestError) {
      setError(`No se pudo registrar la decisión: ${requestError.message}`);
      setBusy(false);
      return;
    }
    setNotice(`${selected.codigo}: ${approve ? "aprobado y liberado" : "devuelto a Packing con observaciones"}.`);
    setSelectedId(null);
    setReviewNote("");
    setSignedUrl("");
    await loadDispatches();
    setBusy(false);
  }

  return <section className="space-y-5" aria-labelledby="dispatch-review-title">
    <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
      <div><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.16em] text-[#2D6A4F]">Flujo operativo real</p><h2 id="dispatch-review-title" className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-[#17251D]">Revisión de despachos</h2><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#526157]">Expedientes enviados desde Packing, con informes privados y trazabilidad de la decisión.</p></div>
      <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => void loadDispatches()} disabled={loading}><RefreshCw size={16} className={loading ? "animate-spin" : ""} /> Actualizar</Button>
    </div>

    <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
      <Summary label="Despachos" value={counts.total} icon={FileCheck2} />
      <Summary label="Lotes bajo supervisión" value={operationTotal} icon={Package} />
      <Summary label="Pendientes" value={counts.pending} icon={Clock3} tone="amber" />
      <Summary label="Aprobados" value={counts.approved} icon={Check} tone="green" />
      <Summary label="Devueltos" value={counts.rejected} icon={AlertCircle} tone="red" />
    </div>

    <Card>
      <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle>Estado de expedientes</CardTitle><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#526157]">Distribución de todos los despachos cargados desde Supabase.</p></CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
        {(["PENDIENTE_FIRMA", "APROBADO", "RECHAZADO"] as const).map((state) => {
          const value = dispatches.filter((item) => item.estado === state).length;
          const percentage = counts.total ? (value / counts.total) * 100 : 0;
          const color = state === "APROBADO" ? "bg-green-700" : state === "RECHAZADO" ? "bg-red-600" : "bg-amber-500";
          return <div key={state}>
            <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs"><span className="font-semibold text-[#17251D]">{stateLabels[state]}</span><span className="tabular-nums text-[#526157]">{value} · {Math.round(percentage)}%</span></div>
            <div role="img" aria-label={`${stateLabels[state]}: ${value} de ${counts.total}`} className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[#E8EEE7]"><div className={`h-full ${color}`} style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }} /></div>
          </div>;
        })}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>

    <Card>
      <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle>Distribución de lotes por estado</CardTitle><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#526157]">Vista general de los lotes reales registrados en Supabase: {operationTotal} en total.</p></CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        {operationError ? <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-800">{operationError}</p> : operationTotal === 0 && loading ? <p role="status" className="text-sm text-[#526157]">Cargando estados de lote…</p> : operationTotal === 0 ? <p className="text-sm text-[#526157]">No hay lotes registrados para graficar.</p> : <div className="grid items-center gap-6 sm:grid-cols-[200px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
          <div className="mx-auto grid h-48 w-48 place-items-center rounded-full" role="img" aria-label={`Gráfico circular: distribución de ${operationTotal} lotes por estado`} style={{ background: pieBackground }}>
            <div className="grid h-28 w-28 place-content-center rounded-full bg-white text-center shadow-inner"><span className="text-2xl font-bold tabular-nums text-[#17251D]">{operationTotal}</span><span className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase text-[#526157]">lotes</span></div>
          </div>
          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {operationCounts.map(({ estado, total }) => <div key={estado} className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3 border-b border-gray-100 pb-2">
              <span aria-hidden="true" className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0" style={{ backgroundColor: operationStatusColors[estado] }} />
              <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm text-[#526157]">{operationStatusLabels[estado]}</span>
              <span className="text-sm font-bold tabular-nums text-[#17251D]">{total}</span>
              <span className="w-10 text-right text-xs tabular-nums text-gray-500">{Math.round((total / operationTotal) * 100)}%</span>
            </div>)}
          </div>
        </div>}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>

    {error && <Card className="border-red-200 bg-red-50 shadow-none"><CardContent className="p-4 text-sm text-red-900">{error}</CardContent></Card>}
    {notice && <Card className="border-green-200 bg-green-50 shadow-none"><CardContent className="p-4 text-sm text-green-900">{notice}</CardContent></Card>}
    <Card>
      <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle>Expedientes enviados por Packing</CardTitle></CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="p-0">
        {loading && dispatches.length === 0 ? <p role="status" className="px-5 pb-5 text-sm text-[#526157]">Cargando despachos…</p> : dispatches.length === 0 ? <div className="px-5 pb-7 text-center"><FileText size={23} className="mx-auto text-gray-400" /><p className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D]">Aún no hay despachos enviados</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#526157]">Los expedientes aparecerán aquí cuando Packing los envíe.</p></div> : <>
          <div className="grid gap-3 border-y border-gray-100 bg-[#F5F8F4]/60 p-4 sm:grid-cols-[minmax(220px,1fr)_200px_200px]">
            <label className="relative block"><span className="sr-only">Buscar despachos</span><Search size={16} aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#526157]" /><input type="search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Buscar código, lote, fundo…" className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17251D]/15 bg-white pl-10 pr-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20" /></label>
            <label><span className="sr-only">Filtrar por estado</span><select value={statusFilter} onChange={(event) => setStatusFilter(event.target.value as DispatchState | "ALL")} className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17251D]/15 bg-white px-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20"><option value="ALL">Todos los estados</option><option value="PENDIENTE_FIRMA">Pendientes</option><option value="APROBADO">Aprobados</option><option value="RECHAZADO">Devueltos</option></select></label>
            <label className="relative"><span className="sr-only">Ordenar por fecha</span><ArrowDownWideNarrow size={16} aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#526157]" /><select value={dateOrder} onChange={(event) => setDateOrder(event.target.value as "newest" | "oldest")} className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17251D]/15 bg-white pl-10 pr-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20"><option value="newest">Más recientes primero</option><option value="oldest">Más antiguos primero</option></select></label>
          </div>
          {visibleDispatches.length === 0 ? <p className="px-5 py-8 text-center text-sm text-[#526157]">No hay despachos que coincidan con esos filtros.</p> : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto"><table className="w-full min-w-[790px] text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="border-y border-gray-100 bg-[#F5F8F4] text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-[#526157]"><tr><th className="px-5 py-3">Despacho / lote</th><th className="px-3 py-3">Destino</th><th className="px-3 py-3">Cajas</th><th className="px-3 py-3">Informe</th><th className="px-3 py-3">Estado</th><th className="px-5 py-3 text-right">Detalle</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">{visibleDispatches.map((item) => <tr key={item.id} className="hover:bg-[#F5F8F4]/70">
              <td className="px-5 py-4"><p className="font-bold text-[#1B4332]">{item.codigo}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#526157]">{item.lot?.fundo ?? "Lote"} · {item.lot?.cultivo ?? item.lote_id}</p><p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-gray-400">{new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleString("es-PE")}</p></td>
              <td className="px-3 py-4">{item.destino}</td><td className="px-3 py-4 tabular-nums">{item.cajas.toLocaleString("es-PE")}</td>
              <td className="px-3 py-4">{item.document ? <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-[#1B4332]"><FileText size={14} /> Adjuntado</span> : <span className="text-xs text-red-700">Falta informe</span>}</td>
              <td className="px-3 py-4"><span className={`inline-flex px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${item.estado === "APROBADO" ? "bg-green-100 text-green-800" : item.estado === "RECHAZADO" ? "bg-red-100 text-red-800" : "bg-amber-100 text-amber-900"}`}>{stateLabels[item.estado]}</span></td>
              <td className="px-5 py-4 text-right"><Button type="button" variant="outline" className="min-h-9" onClick={() => { setSelectedId(item.id); setReviewNote(""); setSignedUrl(""); setError(""); }}>Revisar</Button></td>
            </tr>)}</tbody>
          </table></div>
          )}
        </>}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>

    <Card>
      <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle>Operaciones por lote</CardTitle><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#526157]">Seguimiento del estado real de cada lote, aunque todavía no tenga un despacho enviado.</p></CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="p-0">
        {operationError ? <p className="px-5 pb-5 text-sm text-red-800">{operationError}</p> : operationLots.length === 0 && loading ? <p role="status" className="px-5 pb-5 text-sm text-[#526157]">Cargando lotes…</p> : operationLots.length === 0 ? <p className="px-5 pb-7 text-sm text-[#526157]">No hay lotes registrados.</p> : <>
          <div className="grid gap-3 border-y border-gray-100 bg-[#F5F8F4]/60 p-4 sm:grid-cols-[minmax(220px,1fr)_210px_200px]">
            <label className="relative block"><span className="sr-only">Buscar operaciones</span><Search size={16} aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#526157]" /><input type="search" value={operationQuery} onChange={(event) => setOperationQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Buscar lote, fundo o cultivo…" className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17251D]/15 bg-white pl-10 pr-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20" /></label>
            <label><span className="sr-only">Filtrar operaciones por estado</span><select value={operationStatus} onChange={(event) => setOperationStatus(event.target.value as OperationStatus | "ALL")} className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17251D]/15 bg-white px-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20"><option value="ALL">Todos los estados</option>{operationStates.map((estado) => <option key={estado} value={estado}>{operationStatusLabels[estado]}</option>)}</select></label>
            <label className="relative block"><span className="sr-only">Ordenar operaciones por fecha</span><ArrowDownWideNarrow size={16} aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#526157]" /><select value={operationDateOrder} onChange={(event) => setOperationDateOrder(event.target.value as "newest" | "oldest")} className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17251D]/15 bg-white pl-10 pr-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20"><option value="newest">Más recientes primero</option><option value="oldest">Más antiguos primero</option></select></label>
          </div>
          {visibleOperationLots.length === 0 ? <p className="px-5 py-8 text-center text-sm text-[#526157]">No hay lotes que coincidan con los filtros.</p> : <>
          <div className="overflow-x-auto"><table className="w-full min-w-[700px] text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="border-y border-gray-100 bg-[#F5F8F4] text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-[#526157]"><tr><th className="px-5 py-3">Lote</th><th className="px-3 py-3">Fundo</th><th className="px-3 py-3">Cultivo</th><th className="px-3 py-3">Estado</th><th className="px-5 py-3 text-right">Registrado</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">{pagedOperationLots.map((lot) => <tr key={lot.id} className="hover:bg-[#F5F8F4]/70"><td className="px-5 py-4 font-semibold text-[#17251D]">{lot.id}</td><td className="px-3 py-4">{lot.fundo}</td><td className="px-3 py-4">{lot.cultivo}</td><td className="px-3 py-4"><span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-[#17251D]"><span className="h-2.5 w-2.5" style={{ backgroundColor: operationStatusColors[lot.estado] }} />{operationStatusLabels[lot.estado]}</span></td><td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-right text-xs text-[#526157]">{new Date(lot.created_at).toLocaleDateString("es-PE")}</td></tr>)}</tbody>
          </table></div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-gray-100 px-4 py-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between"><p className="text-xs text-[#526157]">Mostrando {(currentOperationPage - 1) * operationPageSize + 1}–{Math.min(currentOperationPage * operationPageSize, visibleOperationLots.length)} de {visibleOperationLots.length} lotes</p><div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Button type="button" variant="outline" className="min-h-9 rounded-lg px-3" aria-label="Página anterior de lotes" onClick={() => setOperationPage((value) => Math.max(1, value - 1))} disabled={currentOperationPage === 1}><ChevronLeft size={15} /> Anterior</Button><span className="min-w-16 text-center text-xs tabular-nums text-[#526157]">{currentOperationPage} / {operationPageCount}</span><Button type="button" variant="outline" className="min-h-9 rounded-lg px-3" aria-label="Página siguiente de lotes" onClick={() => setOperationPage((value) => Math.min(operationPageCount, value + 1))} disabled={currentOperationPage >= operationPageCount}>Siguiente <ChevronRight size={15} /></Button></div></div>
          </>}
        </>}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>

    {selected && <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 p-4" onMouseDown={(event) => { if (event.target === event.currentTarget) setSelectedId(null); }}>
      <section role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="dispatch-dialog-title" className="max-h-[90dvh] w-full max-w-2xl overflow-y-auto rounded-xl bg-white shadow-xl">
        <header className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-gray-100 p-5"><div><p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#2D6A4F]">Revisión administrativa</p><h3 id="dispatch-dialog-title" className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-[#17251D]">{selected.codigo}</h3></div><Button type="button" variant="ghost" className="min-h-9 px-2" aria-label="Cerrar detalle" onClick={() => setSelectedId(null)}><X size={17} /></Button></header>
        <div className="space-y-5 p-5">
          <dl className="grid gap-4 text-sm sm:grid-cols-2"><Info label="Lote" value={`${selected.lot?.fundo ?? ""} · ${selected.lot?.cultivo ?? selected.lote_id}`} /><Info label="Destino" value={selected.destino} /><Info label="Cajas" value={selected.cajas.toLocaleString("es-PE")} /><Info label="Enviado" value={new Date(selected.created_at).toLocaleString("es-PE")} /></dl>
          {selected.document ? <div className="border border-gray-200 p-4"><p className="text-sm font-bold text-[#17251D]">{selected.document.nombre_archivo}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#526157]">{(selected.document.size_bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB · acceso privado temporal</p><div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3"><Button type="button" variant="outline" className="min-h-9" onClick={() => void createDocumentLink(selected)}><FileText size={15} /> Preparar descarga segura</Button>{signedUrl && <a href={signedUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#1B4332] underline">Abrir informe</a>}</div></div> : <p className="border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-900">Este expediente no tiene un informe asociado; no puede aprobarse.</p>}
          {selected.estado !== "PENDIENTE_FIRMA" ? <div className="border border-gray-200 bg-[#F5F8F4] p-4 text-sm"><p className="font-semibold">{stateLabels[selected.estado]}</p>{selected.motivo_revision && <p className="mt-2 text-[#526157]">Motivo: {selected.motivo_revision}</p>}{selected.revisado_at && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">Revisado {new Date(selected.revisado_at).toLocaleString("es-PE")}</p>}</div> : <>
            <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D]">Observación de revisión<textarea value={reviewNote} onChange={(event) => setReviewNote(event.target.value)} maxLength={500} rows={3} placeholder="Obligatoria para devolver a Packing…" className="mt-2 w-full resize-y rounded-xl border border-gray-200 p-3 text-sm font-normal outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20" /></label>
            {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-800">{error}</p>}
            <div className="flex flex-col-reverse justify-end gap-2 sm:flex-row"><Button type="button" variant="outline" disabled={busy || !selected.document} onClick={() => void resolve(false)}><X size={15} /> Devolver a Packing</Button><Button type="button" disabled={busy || !selected.document} onClick={() => void resolve(true)}><ShieldCheck size={15} /> Aprobar y liberar</Button></div>
          </>}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>}
  </section>;
}

function Summary({ label, value, icon: Icon, tone = "forest" }: { label: string; value: number; icon: typeof FileCheck2; tone?: "forest" | "amber" | "green" | "red" }) {
  const tones = { forest: "bg-[#E8EEE7] text-[#1B4332]", amber: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-900", green: "bg-green-100 text-green-900", red: "bg-red-100 text-red-900" };
  return <Card><CardContent className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 p-5"><div><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#526157]">{label}</p><p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold tabular-nums text-[#17251D]">{value.toLocaleString("es-PE")}</p></div><span className={`flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center ${tones[tone]}`}><Icon size={18} /></span></CardContent></Card>;
}

function Info({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return <div><dt className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-400">{label}</dt><dd className="mt-1 break-words font-semibold text-[#17251D]">{value}</dd></div>;
}

"use client";

import { RoleAnalyticsDashboard } from "@/features/dashboards/RoleAnalyticsDashboard";

==> src/features/gerencia/dashboard-data.ts <==
export const dashboardStatuses = ["EN_CAMPO", "EN_PACKING", "PENDIENTE_FIRMA", "APROBADO", "RECHAZADO"] as const;
export type DashboardStatus = typeof dashboardStatuses[number];
export type DashboardPeriod = "today" | "7d" | "30d" | "90d" | "all" | "custom";
export const dashboardStatusConfig: Record<DashboardStatus, { label: string; color: string }> = {
  EN_CAMPO: { label: "En campo", color: "var(--status-field)" },
  EN_PACKING: { label: "En Packing", color: "var(--status-packing)" },
  PENDIENTE_FIRMA: { label: "Pendiente de firma", color: "var(--status-pending)" },
  APROBADO: { label: "Aprobado", color: "var(--status-approved)" },
  RECHAZADO: { label: "Rechazado", color: "var(--status-rejected)" },
};
export function categoryColor(label: string): string {
  let hash = 0;
  let hueHash = 2166136261;
  for (const character of label.normalize("NFC").toLocaleLowerCase("es")) {
    const codePoint = character.codePointAt(0);
    if (codePoint !== undefined) {
      hash = (hash * 31 + codePoint) >>> 0;
      hueHash = Math.imul(hueHash ^ codePoint, 16777619) >>> 0;
    }
  }
  const hue = hueHash % 360;
  const saturation = 48 + (hash % 13);
  const lightness = 38 + ((hash >>> 8) % 9);
  return `hsl(${hue} ${saturation}% ${lightness}%)`;
}

export type DashboardLot = {
  id: string;
  fundo: string;
  cultivo: string;
  agroquimico: string;
  estado: DashboardStatus;
  fecha_cosecha: string;
  created_at: string;
  destino?: string | null;
  creado_por?: string;
};

export type GerenciaDashboardData = {
  lotes: DashboardLot[];
  documentos: number;
};

const limaDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
  timeZone: "America/Lima",
  year: "numeric",
  month: "2-digit",
  day: "2-digit",
});

export function dateKeyInLima(value: Date | string): string {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) throw new Error("Fecha no válida.");
  const parts = limaDate.formatToParts(date);
  const year = parts.find((part) => part.type === "year")?.value;
  const month = parts.find((part) => part.type === "month")?.value;
  const day = parts.find((part) => part.type === "day")?.value;
  if (!year || !month || !day) throw new Error("No se pudo calcular la fecha local.");
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
}

export function shiftDateKey(dateKey: string, days: number): string {
  const [year, month, day] = dateKey.split("-").map(Number);
  const shifted = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day + days));
  return shifted.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

export function dashboardSinceDate(period: DashboardPeriod, now = new Date()): string | null {
  if (period === "all" || period === "custom") return null;
  if (period === "today") return dateKeyInLima(now);
  const days = period === "7d" ? 7 : period === "30d" ? 30 : 90;
  return shiftDateKey(dateKeyInLima(now), -(days - 1));
}

export function parseGerenciaDashboard(value: unknown): GerenciaDashboardData {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") throw new Error("Formato de panel no válido.");
  const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (!Array.isArray(record.lotes) || typeof record.documentos !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(record.documentos) || record.documentos < 0) {
    throw new Error("Formato de panel no válido.");
  }

  const lotes = record.lotes.map((value): DashboardLot => {
    if (!value || typeof value !== "object") throw new Error("Formato de lote no válido.");
    const row = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (
      typeof row.id !== "string"
      || typeof row.fundo !== "string"
      || typeof row.cultivo !== "string"
      || typeof row.agroquimico !== "string"
      || typeof row.estado !== "string"
      || !dashboardStatuses.includes(row.estado as DashboardStatus)
      || typeof row.fecha_cosecha !== "string"
      || typeof row.created_at !== "string"
      || Number.isNaN(Date.parse(row.created_at))
    ) {
      throw new Error("Formato de lote no válido.");
    }
    return {
      id: row.id,
      fundo: row.fundo,
      cultivo: row.cultivo,
      agroquimico: row.agroquimico,
      estado: row.estado as DashboardStatus,
      fecha_cosecha: row.fecha_cosecha,
      created_at: row.created_at,
      destino: typeof row.destino === "string" ? row.destino : null,
      creado_por: typeof row.creado_por === "string" ? row.creado_por : undefined,
    };
  });
  return { lotes, documentos: record.documentos };
}

export function getStatusCounts(lotes: DashboardLot[]) {
  return dashboardStatuses.map((estado) => ({
    estado,
    total: lotes.filter((lote) => lote.estado === estado).length,
  }));
}

export type RegistrationBucket = { key: string; label: string; fullLabel: string; total: number };

export function getRegistrationSeries(
  lotes: DashboardLot[],
  startDate: string | null,
  endDate: string,
  granularity: "day" | "week",
  coverageStart?: string | null,
): RegistrationBucket[] {
  const observedDates = lotes.map((lote) => dateKeyInLima(lote.created_at)).sort();
  const firstObserved = coverageStart ?? observedDates[0];
  if (!firstObserved) return [];
  const start = startDate && startDate > firstObserved ? startDate : firstObserved;
  if (start > endDate) return [];

  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const lote of lotes) {
    const date = dateKeyInLima(lote.created_at);
    if (date < start || date > endDate) continue;
    const [year, month, day] = date.split("-").map(Number);
    const weekday = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day)).getUTCDay();
    const bucket = granularity === "day" ? date : shiftDateKey(date, -((weekday + 6) % 7));
    counts.set(bucket, (counts.get(bucket) ?? 0) + 1);
  }

  const output: RegistrationBucket[] = [];
  let cursor = granularity === "week"
    ? shiftDateKey(start, -((new Date(`${start}T12:00:00Z`).getUTCDay() + 6) % 7))
    : start;
  const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-PE", {
    timeZone: "America/Lima",
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
  });
  while (cursor <= endDate) {
    const timestamp = new Date(`${cursor}T12:00:00Z`);
    const endOfBucket = granularity === "week" ? shiftDateKey(cursor, 6) : cursor;
    const coveredStart = cursor < start ? start : cursor;
    const coveredEnd = endOfBucket > endDate ? endDate : endOfBucket;
    const labelTimestamp = new Date(`${coveredStart}T12:00:00Z`);
    const fullLabel = granularity === "week"
      ? `${coveredStart !== cursor || coveredEnd !== endOfBucket ? "Semana parcial · " : ""}${formatter.format(labelTimestamp)} – ${formatter.format(new Date(`${coveredEnd}T12:00:00Z`))}`
      : formatter.format(labelTimestamp);
    output.push({
      key: cursor,
      label: formatter.format(labelTimestamp),
      fullLabel,
      total: counts.get(cursor) ?? 0,
    });
    cursor = shiftDateKey(cursor, granularity === "week" ? 7 : 1);
  }
  return output;
}

export function comparePeriodCounts(current: number, previous: number) {
  const difference = current - previous;
  return {
    difference,
    label: previous === 0
      ? `Sin base de comparación · ${difference >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${difference}`
      : `${difference >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${difference} vs. período anterior`,
  };
}

==> src/features/gerencia/dashboard-export.ts <==
import type { DashboardLot } from "./dashboard-data";

const exportStatusLabels: Record<DashboardLot["estado"], string> = {
  EN_CAMPO: "En campo",
  EN_PACKING: "En Packing",
  PENDIENTE_FIRMA: "Pendiente de firma",
  APROBADO: "Aprobado",
  RECHAZADO: "Rechazado",
};

function csvCell(value: string) {
  const safeValue = /^[\s]*[=+\-@]/.test(value) ? `'${value}` : value;
  return `"${safeValue.replaceAll('"', '""')}"`;
}

function formatLimaTimestamp(value: string) {
  return new Date(value).toLocaleString("es-PE", {
    timeZone: "America/Lima",
    dateStyle: "short",
    timeStyle: "short",
  });
}

export function dashboardLotsCsv(lots: DashboardLot[]) {
  const rows = [
    ["Lote", "Fundo", "Cultivo", "Agroquímico", "Estado", "Destino", "Fecha de cosecha", "Registrado (America/Lima)", "Origen"],
    ...lots.map((lot) => [
      lot.id,
      lot.fundo,
      lot.cultivo,
      lot.agroquimico,
      exportStatusLabels[lot.estado],
      lot.destino === "USA" ? "Estados Unidos" : lot.destino === "UE" ? "Unión Europea" : lot.destino ?? "Sin destino",
      lot.fecha_cosecha,
      formatLimaTimestamp(lot.created_at),
      lot.fundo.startsWith("DEMO") ? "DEMO" : "No marcado DEMO",
    ]),
  ];
  return `\uFEFF${rows.map((row) => row.map(csvCell).join(",")).join("\r\n")}`;
}

export function dashboardCsvFilename(date: string) {
  return `lotes-filtrados-${date}.csv`;
}

==> src/features/profile/ProfileAvatar.tsx <==
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback, AvatarImage } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { getSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";
import type { UserProfile } from "@/features/auth/roles";

export function profileInitials(name: string) {
  return name.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 2).map((part) => part[0] ?? "").join("").toUpperCase() || "?";
}

export function ProfileAvatar({ profile, className }: { profile: UserProfile; className?: string }) {
  const [imageUrl, setImageUrl] = useState("");
  const [imageFailed, setImageFailed] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setImageUrl("");
    setImageFailed(false);
    if (!profile.avatar_path) return () => { active = false; };
    const client = getSupabaseBrowserClient();
    if (!client) return () => { active = false; };
    void client.storage.from("user-avatars").createSignedUrl(profile.avatar_path, 3600)
      .then(({ data, error }) => {
        if (!active) return;
        if (error) {
          console.error("No se pudo generar la URL firmada del avatar.", error);
          return;
        }
        setImageUrl(data.signedUrl);
      })
      .catch((cause: unknown) => {
        if (active) console.error("No se pudo generar la URL firmada del avatar.", cause);
      });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [profile.avatar_path]);

  return (
    <Avatar className={className}>
      {imageUrl && !imageFailed ? <AvatarImage src={imageUrl} alt={`Foto de ${profile.nombre}`} onError={() => setImageFailed(true)} /> : <AvatarFallback>{profileInitials(profile.nombre)}</AvatarFallback>}
    </Avatar>
  );
}

==> src/features/profile/ProfileDialog.tsx <==
"use client";

import type { FormEvent, KeyboardEvent } from "react";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import { Camera, Check, LoaderCircle, Pencil, Trash2, X } from "lucide-react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback, AvatarImage } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { roleAreas, roleLabels, type UserProfile } from "@/features/auth/roles";
import { validateAvatarFile, validateAvatarSignature, validateEditableProfile } from "@/features/profile/profile-validation";
import { getSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";
import { profileInitials } from "./ProfileAvatar";

type Props = {
  profile: UserProfile;
  startInEditMode: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onProfileSaved: (updates: Pick<UserProfile, "nombre" | "telefono" | "descripcion" | "avatar_path">) => void;
};

export function ProfileDialog({ profile, startInEditMode, onClose, onProfileSaved }: Props) {
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(startInEditMode);
  const [name, setName] = useState(profile.nombre);
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(profile.telefono ?? "");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(profile.descripcion ?? "");
  const [selectedAvatar, setSelectedAvatar] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [previewUrl, setPreviewUrl] = useState("");
  const [removeAvatar, setRemoveAvatar] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");
  const [warning, setWarning] = useState("");
  const fileInput = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const dialog = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion();

  const dirty = useMemo(() => (
    name !== profile.nombre
    || phone !== (profile.telefono ?? "")
    || description !== (profile.descripcion ?? "")
    || selectedAvatar !== null
    || removeAvatar
  ), [description, name, phone, profile.descripcion, profile.nombre, profile.telefono, removeAvatar, selectedAvatar]);

  useEffect(() => {
    dialog.current?.focus();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedAvatar) {
      setPreviewUrl("");
      return;
    }
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(selectedAvatar);
    setPreviewUrl(url);
    return () => URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }, [selectedAvatar]);

  function requestClose() {
    if (saving) return;
    if (editing && dirty && !window.confirm("Tienes cambios sin guardar. ¿Quieres descartarlos?")) return;
    onClose();
  }

  function handleDialogKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    if (event.key === "Escape") {
      event.preventDefault();
      requestClose();
      return;
    }
    if (event.key !== "Tab" || !dialog.current) return;
    const focusable = dialog.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
      'button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])',
    );
    if (!focusable.length) return;
    const first = focusable[0];
    const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
    if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
      event.preventDefault();
      last.focus();
    } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
      event.preventDefault();
      first.focus();
    }
  }

  function beginEdit() {
    setName(profile.nombre);
    setPhone(profile.telefono ?? "");
    setDescription(profile.descripcion ?? "");
    setSelectedAvatar(null);
    setRemoveAvatar(false);
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");
    setWarning("");
    setEditing(true);
  }

  function cancelEdit() {
    if (dirty && !window.confirm("Tienes cambios sin guardar. ¿Quieres descartarlos?")) return;
    setName(profile.nombre);
    setPhone(profile.telefono ?? "");
    setDescription(profile.descripcion ?? "");
    setSelectedAvatar(null);
    setRemoveAvatar(false);
    setError("");
    setWarning("");
    setEditing(false);
  }

  async function handleAvatarSelection(file: File | undefined) {
    if (!file) return;
    const fileError = validateAvatarFile(file);
    if (fileError) {
      setError(fileError);
      if (fileInput.current) fileInput.current.value = "";
      return;
    }
    try {
      if (!await validateAvatarSignature(file)) {
        setError("El contenido no coincide con JPEG, PNG o WebP. No se aceptan archivos SVG.");
        if (fileInput.current) fileInput.current.value = "";
        return;
      }
      const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(file);
      bitmap.close();
    } catch {
      setError("No se pudo leer la imagen. Elige un archivo JPEG, PNG o WebP válido.");
      if (fileInput.current) fileInput.current.value = "";
      return;
    }
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");
    setSelectedAvatar(file);
    setRemoveAvatar(false);
  }

  async function saveProfile(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");
    setWarning("");
    const validationError = validateEditableProfile({ nombre: name, telefono: phone, descripcion: description });
    if (validationError) {
      setError(validationError);
      return;
    }

    const client = getSupabaseBrowserClient();
    if (!client) {
      setError("No está disponible la conexión con Supabase. No se guardaron los cambios.");
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    let uploadedPath: string | null = null;
    let profileSaved = false;
    const oldAvatarPath = profile.avatar_path;
    let nextAvatarPath = removeAvatar ? null : oldAvatarPath;
    try {
      if (selectedAvatar) {
        const extension = selectedAvatar.type === "image/jpeg" ? "jpg" : selectedAvatar.type === "image/png" ? "png" : "webp";
        const newPath = `${profile.id}/${crypto.randomUUID()}.${extension}`;
        const { error: uploadError } = await client.storage.from("user-avatars").upload(newPath, selectedAvatar, {
          cacheControl: "3600",
          contentType: selectedAvatar.type,
          upsert: false,
        });
        if (uploadError) throw new Error("No se pudo subir la foto. Verifica tu conexión y vuelve a intentarlo.");
        uploadedPath = newPath;
        nextAvatarPath = newPath;
      }

      const { error: saveError } = await client.rpc("actualizar_perfil_propio", {
        p_nombre: name.trim(),
        p_telefono: phone.trim() || null,
        p_descripcion: description.trim() || null,
        p_avatar_path: nextAvatarPath,
      });
      if (saveError) throw new Error(saveError.message === "account_not_active"
        ? "Tu cuenta ya no está habilitada y no se pudieron guardar los cambios."
        : "No se pudo guardar el perfil. Revisa los datos e inténtalo nuevamente.");

      profileSaved = true;
      onProfileSaved({
        nombre: name.trim(),
        telefono: phone.trim() || null,
        descripcion: description.trim() || null,
        avatar_path: nextAvatarPath,
      });
      setSelectedAvatar(null);
      setRemoveAvatar(false);
      setEditing(false);
      setSuccess("Perfil actualizado correctamente.");

      if (oldAvatarPath && oldAvatarPath !== nextAvatarPath) {
        try {
          const { error: removeError } = await client.storage.from("user-avatars").remove([oldAvatarPath]);
          if (removeError) setWarning("El perfil se guardó, pero no se pudo quitar la foto anterior del almacenamiento.");
        } catch {
          setWarning("El perfil se guardó, pero no se pudo confirmar la eliminación de la foto anterior.");
        }
      }
    } catch (cause) {
      if (profileSaved) {
        setWarning("El perfil se guardó, pero ocurrió un problema al limpiar la foto anterior.");
        return;
      }
      if (uploadedPath) {
        try {
          const { error: cleanupError } = await client.storage.from("user-avatars").remove([uploadedPath]);
          if (cleanupError) throw cleanupError;
        } catch {
          setError("No se pudo guardar el perfil y tampoco limpiar la foto temporal. Contacta a soporte para retirar el archivo.");
          return;
        }
      }
      setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "No se pudo guardar el perfil. Inténtalo nuevamente.");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  const photoUrl = previewUrl || "";

  return createPortal(
    <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} transition={{ duration: reduceMotion ? 0 : 0.16 }} className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] grid place-items-center overflow-y-auto bg-[#17251D]/55 p-3 sm:p-6" onMouseDown={(event) => {
      if (event.target === event.currentTarget) requestClose();
    }}>
      <motion.section
        ref={dialog}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="profile-dialog-title"
        tabIndex={-1}
        onKeyDown={handleDialogKeyDown}
        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: reduceMotion ? 0 : 6 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
        transition={{ duration: reduceMotion ? 0 : 0.16 }}
        className="my-auto max-h-[min(calc(100dvh-1.5rem),800px)] w-full max-w-xl overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-[#17251D]/10 bg-white shadow-2xl outline-none sm:max-h-[min(calc(100dvh-3rem),800px)]"
      >
        <header className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[#17251D]/10 px-5 py-5 sm:px-7">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.15em] text-[#2D6A4F]">Cuenta personal</p>
            <h2 id="profile-dialog-title" className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold text-[#17251D]">{editing ? "Editar perfil" : "Mi perfil"}</h2>
          </div>
          <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label="Cerrar perfil" className="min-h-9 rounded-lg px-2" onClick={requestClose}><X size={18} /></Button>
        </header>

        <div className="p-5 sm:p-7">
          {editing ? (
            <form onSubmit={(event) => void saveProfile(event)} className="space-y-5">
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 sm:flex-row">
                <Avatar className="h-20 w-20 border-2 border-[#E5ECE5] bg-[#2D6A4F] text-lg">
                  {photoUrl ? <AvatarImage src={photoUrl} alt="Vista previa de la foto de perfil" /> : profile.avatar_path && !removeAvatar ? <CurrentProfileImage profile={profile} /> : <AvatarFallback>{profileInitials(name || profile.nombre)}</AvatarFallback>}
                </Avatar>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2 sm:justify-start">
                  <input
                    ref={fileInput}
                    type="file"
                    accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp"
                    aria-label="Seleccionar foto de perfil"
                    className="sr-only"
                    onChange={(event) => void handleAvatarSelection(event.target.files?.[0])}
                  />
                  <Button type="button" variant="outline" className="rounded-xl" onClick={() => fileInput.current?.click()}>
                    <Camera size={16} /> {profile.avatar_path && !removeAvatar || selectedAvatar ? "Cambiar foto" : "Agregar foto"}
                  </Button>
                  {(profile.avatar_path && !removeAvatar || selectedAvatar) && (
                    <Button type="button" variant="ghost" className="rounded-xl text-red-800 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-900" onClick={() => {
                      setSelectedAvatar(null);
                      setRemoveAvatar(true);
                      setError("");
                    }}>
                      <Trash2 size={15} /> Quitar foto
                    </Button>
                  )}
                  <p className="w-full text-center text-xs text-[#526157] sm:text-left">JPEG, PNG o WebP · máximo 2 MB</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D]">
                Nombre <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-red-700">*</span>
                <Input value={name} onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)} required maxLength={120} autoComplete="name" className="mt-2 rounded-xl" />
              </label>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D]">
                Correo electrónico
                <Input type="email" value={profile.email} readOnly aria-readonly="true" className="mt-2 rounded-xl bg-[#F5F8F4] text-[#526157]" />
                <span className="mt-1 block text-xs font-normal text-[#526157]">El correo se administra desde Supabase Auth y no se cambia en este formulario.</span>
              </label>

              <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D]">
                  Rol
                  <Input value={roleLabels[profile.rol]} readOnly aria-readonly="true" className="mt-2 rounded-xl bg-[#F5F8F4] text-[#526157]" />
                </label>
                <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D]">
                  Área de trabajo
                  <Input value={roleAreas[profile.rol]} readOnly aria-readonly="true" className="mt-2 rounded-xl bg-[#F5F8F4] text-[#526157]" />
                </label>
              </div>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D]">
                Teléfono <span className="font-normal text-[#526157]">(opcional)</span>
                <Input type="tel" value={phone} onChange={(event) => setPhone(event.target.value)} maxLength={25} autoComplete="tel" className="mt-2 rounded-xl" placeholder="+51 999 999 999" />
              </label>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D]">
                Descripción personal <span className="font-normal text-[#526157]">(opcional)</span>
                <textarea value={description} onChange={(event) => setDescription(event.target.value)} maxLength={280} rows={3} className="mt-2 w-full resize-y rounded-xl border border-[#17251D]/20 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-[#17251D] outline-none focus:border-[#2D6A4F] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#2D6A4F]/20" aria-describedby="description-count" />
                <span id="description-count" className="mt-1 block text-right text-xs font-normal text-[#526157]">{description.length}/280</span>
              </label>

              {error && <p role="alert" className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-red-900">{error}</p>}

              <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 border-t border-[#17251D]/10 pt-4 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
                <Button type="button" variant="outline" className="rounded-xl" disabled={saving} onClick={cancelEdit}>Cancelar</Button>
                <Button type="submit" className="rounded-xl" disabled={saving}>
                  {saving ? <><LoaderCircle className="animate-spin" size={16} /> Guardando…</> : <><Check size={16} /> Guardar cambios</>}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <Avatar className="h-20 w-20 bg-[#2D6A4F] text-lg">
                  <CurrentProfileImage profile={profile} />
                </Avatar>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <p className="break-words text-xl font-bold text-[#17251D]">{profile.nombre}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 break-all text-sm text-[#526157]">{profile.email}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <dl className="grid gap-4 rounded-2xl bg-[#F5F8F4] p-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <ProfileDetail label="Rol" value={roleLabels[profile.rol]} />
                <ProfileDetail label="Área de trabajo" value={roleAreas[profile.rol]} />
                <ProfileDetail label="Teléfono" value={profile.telefono || "No indicado"} />
                <ProfileDetail label="Descripción" value={profile.descripcion || "No indicada"} />
              </dl>
              {success && <p role="status" className="rounded-xl border border-green-200 bg-green-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-green-900">{success}</p>}
              {warning && <p role="status" className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-amber-950">{warning}</p>}
              <Button type="button" className="w-full rounded-xl sm:w-auto" onClick={beginEdit}><Pencil size={16} /> Editar perfil</Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </motion.section>
    </motion.div>,
    document.body,
  );
}

function CurrentProfileImage({ profile }: { profile: UserProfile }) {
  const [url, setUrl] = useState("");
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setUrl("");
    setFailed(false);
    if (!profile.avatar_path) return () => { active = false; };
    const client = getSupabaseBrowserClient();
    if (!client) return () => { active = false; };
    void client.storage.from("user-avatars").createSignedUrl(profile.avatar_path, 3600)
      .then(({ data, error }) => {
        if (!active) return;
        if (error) {
          console.error("No se pudo generar la URL firmada del avatar.", error);
          setFailed(true);
          return;
        }
        setUrl(data.signedUrl);
      })
      .catch((cause: unknown) => {
        if (active) {
          console.error("No se pudo generar la URL firmada del avatar.", cause);
          setFailed(true);
        }
      });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [profile.avatar_path]);

  if (!profile.avatar_path || failed || !url) return <AvatarFallback>{profileInitials(profile.nombre)}</AvatarFallback>;
  return <AvatarImage src={url} alt={`Foto de ${profile.nombre}`} onError={() => setFailed(true)} />;
}

function ProfileDetail({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return <div className="min-w-0">
    <dt className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#526157]">{label}</dt>
    <dd className="mt-1 break-words text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D]">{value}</dd>
  </div>;
}

==> src/features/profile/ProfileMenu.tsx <==
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ChevronDown, LogOut, UserRound, UserRoundPen } from "lucide-react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { useAuth } from "@/features/auth/AuthProvider";
import { ProfileAvatar } from "@/features/profile/ProfileAvatar";
import { ProfileDialog } from "@/features/profile/ProfileDialog";
import { ThemeSelect } from "@/features/theme/ThemeSelect";

export function ProfileMenu() {
  const { profile, signOut, updateProfile } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();
  const trigger = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const menu = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [dialogMode, setDialogMode] = useState<"view" | "edit" | null>(null);
  const [signingOut, setSigningOut] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const restoreFocus = useRef(false);
  const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!menuOpen) return;
    menu.current?.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[role="menuitem"]')?.focus();
    function handlePointerDown(event: PointerEvent) {
      if (event.target instanceof Node && !menu.current?.contains(event.target) && !trigger.current?.contains(event.target)) {
        setMenuOpen(false);
      }
    }
    function handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        event.preventDefault();
        setMenuOpen(false);
        restoreFocus.current = true;
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", handlePointerDown);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", handlePointerDown);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [menuOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (restoreFocus.current && !menuOpen && !dialogMode) {
      trigger.current?.focus();
      restoreFocus.current = false;
    }
  }, [dialogMode, menuOpen]);

  if (!profile) return null;

  function openDialog(mode: "view" | "edit") {
    setMenuOpen(false);
    setDialogMode(mode);
    setError("");
  }

  async function handleSignOut() {
    setSigningOut(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      await signOut();
      router.replace("/login");
    } catch {
      setError("No se pudo cerrar sesión. Inténtalo nuevamente.");
      setSigningOut(false);
    }
  }

  function closeDialog() {
    setDialogMode(null);
    restoreFocus.current = true;
  }

  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <button
        ref={trigger}
        type="button"
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        aria-expanded={menuOpen}
        aria-controls="account-menu"
        aria-label={`Cuenta de ${profile.nombre}`}
        onClick={() => setMenuOpen((open) => !open)}
        className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[#17251D] outline-none transition hover:bg-[#F5F8F4] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#2D6A4F] focus-visible:ring-offset-2"
      >
        <ProfileAvatar profile={profile} className="h-9 w-9 bg-[#2D6A4F]" />
        <span className="hidden max-w-48 truncate text-sm font-bold sm:inline">{profile.nombre}</span>
        <ChevronDown aria-hidden="true" size={15} className={`hidden text-[#526157] transition sm:block ${menuOpen ? "rotate-180" : ""}`} />
      </button>

      {menuOpen && (
        <motion.div ref={menu} id="account-menu" role="menu" aria-label="Menú de cuenta" initial={{ opacity: 0, y: reduceMotion ? 0 : -4 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ duration: reduceMotion ? 0 : 0.12 }} className="absolute right-0 top-full z-40 mt-2 w-72 rounded-2xl border border-default bg-menu p-2 shadow-xl">
          <div className="border-b border-default px-3 py-2">
            <p className="truncate text-sm font-bold text-primary">{profile.nombre}</p>
            <p className="truncate text-xs text-secondary">{profile.email}</p>
          </div>
          <div role="group" aria-label="Preferencia de apariencia" className="px-2 py-2">
            <ThemeSelect compact />
          </div>
          <button role="menuitem" type="button" onClick={() => openDialog("view")} className="flex min-h-11 w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D] outline-none hover:bg-[#F5F8F4] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#2D6A4F]">
            <UserRound size={17} /> Mi perfil
          </button>
          <button role="menuitem" type="button" onClick={() => openDialog("edit")} className="flex min-h-11 w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-[#17251D] outline-none hover:bg-[#F5F8F4] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#2D6A4F]">
            <UserRoundPen size={17} /> Editar perfil
          </button>
          <button role="menuitem" type="button" disabled={signingOut} onClick={() => void handleSignOut()} className="flex min-h-11 w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-red-800 outline-none hover:bg-red-50 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-red-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-100 disabled:text-[#526157]">
            <LogOut size={17} /> {signingOut ? "Cerrando sesión…" : "Cerrar sesión"}
          </button>
          {error && <p role="alert" className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-red-800">{error}</p>}
        </motion.div>
      )}

      {dialogMode && (
        <ProfileDialog
          profile={profile}
          startInEditMode={dialogMode === "edit"}
          onClose={closeDialog}
          onProfileSaved={updateProfile}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

==> src/features/profile/profile-validation.ts <==
export type EditableProfile = {
  nombre: string;
  telefono: string;
  descripcion: string;
};

const avatarTypes = new Set(["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"]);
const maxAvatarBytes = 2 * 1024 * 1024;

export function validateEditableProfile(profile: EditableProfile): string | null {
  const name = profile.nombre.trim();
  if (!name) return "Escribe tu nombre.";
  if (name.length > 120) return "El nombre no puede superar los 120 caracteres.";

  const phone = profile.telefono.trim();
  if (phone) {
    const digits = phone.replace(/\D/g, "");
    if (phone.length < 7 || phone.length > 25 || !/^[+0-9() -]+$/.test(phone) || digits.length < 7 || digits.length > 15) {
      return "Ingresa un teléfono válido de 7 a 15 dígitos.";
    }
  }

  if (profile.descripcion.trim().length > 280) return "La descripción no puede superar los 280 caracteres.";
  return null;
}

export function validateAvatarFile(file: File): string | null {
  if (!avatarTypes.has(file.type)) return "La foto debe ser JPEG, PNG o WebP.";
  if (file.size === 0 || file.size > maxAvatarBytes) return "La foto debe pesar más de 0 y como máximo 2 MB.";
  return null;
}

export async function validateAvatarSignature(file: File): Promise<boolean> {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await file.slice(0, 12).arrayBuffer());
  if (file.type === "image/jpeg") return bytes[0] === 0xff && bytes[1] === 0xd8 && bytes[2] === 0xff;
  if (file.type === "image/png") return bytes[0] === 0x89 && bytes[1] === 0x50 && bytes[2] === 0x4e && bytes[3] === 0x47 && bytes[4] === 0x0d && bytes[5] === 0x0a && bytes[6] === 0x1a && bytes[7] === 0x0a;
  if (file.type === "image/webp") return String.fromCharCode(...bytes.slice(0, 4)) === "RIFF" && String.fromCharCode(...bytes.slice(8, 12)) === "WEBP";
  return false;
}

==> src/features/theme/ThemeProvider.tsx <==
"use client";

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { normalizeThemePreference, resolveTheme, themeStorageKey, type ThemePreference } from "./theme-preference";

type ThemeContextValue = {
  preference: ThemePreference;
  setPreference: (preference: ThemePreference) => void;
};

const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextValue | null>(null);

function applyTheme(preference: ThemePreference, systemIsDark: boolean) {
  document.documentElement.dataset.theme = resolveTheme(preference, systemIsDark);
}

export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [preference, setPreferenceState] = useState<ThemePreference>("system");
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let storedPreference: ThemePreference = "system";
    try {
      storedPreference = normalizeThemePreference(window.localStorage.getItem(themeStorageKey));
    } catch {
      // The current tab still supports theme changes when storage is unavailable.
    }
    setPreferenceState(storedPreference);
    setReady(true);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ready) return;
    const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
    const update = () => applyTheme(preference, media.matches);
    update();
    media.addEventListener("change", update);
    return () => media.removeEventListener("change", update);
  }, [preference, ready]);

  const setPreference = useCallback((nextPreference: ThemePreference) => {
    setPreferenceState(nextPreference);
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(themeStorageKey, nextPreference);
    } catch {
      // Keep the selected appearance for this tab even if persistence is blocked.
    }
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo(() => ({ preference, setPreference }), [preference, setPreference]);
  return <ThemeContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</ThemeContext.Provider>;
}

export function useTheme() {
  const context = useContext(ThemeContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error("ThemeProvider no está montado.");
  return context;
}

"use client";

import { Moon, Sun, SunMoon } from "lucide-react";
import { useTheme } from "./ThemeProvider";
import { normalizeThemePreference, type ThemePreference } from "./theme-preference";

const options: { value: ThemePreference; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "light", label: "Claro" },
  { value: "dark", label: "Oscuro" },
  { value: "system", label: "Usar configuración del sistema" },

export function ThemeSelect({ compact = false }: { compact?: boolean }) {
  const { preference, setPreference } = useTheme();
  const Icon = preference === "dark" ? Moon : preference === "light" ? Sun : SunMoon;

  return (
    <label className={`flex items-center gap-2 ${compact ? "min-w-0" : "rounded-xl border border-default bg-surface px-3 py-2.5"}`}>
      <Icon aria-hidden="true" size={16} className="shrink-0 text-accent" />

        aria-label="Apariencia"
        value={preference}

        ))}
